    """x of the RIGHT edge (TR -> BR) of the convex quad q = [TL, TR, BR, BL] at height y (above / below the edge = its end point)."""
    a, b = q[1], q[2]
    if y <= a[1]:
        return a[0]
    if y >= b[1]:
        return b[0]
    return a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * (y - a[1]) / ((b[1] - a[1]) or 1e-9)


def _el_side_fit(quads, W, ar, cap_w, yt, yb, side="right", gap=EL_SIDE_GAP, edge=EL_SIDE_EDGE):
    """Largest card (aspect ar = w / h) for the free column beside the frame. quads = frame quads [TL, TR, BR, BL] that must stay
    clear (quads[0] = the full-size frame: its edge slope sets the card's shear); the column = between the frame edge (+ gap)
    and the canvas edge (- edge), from yt to yb, the card centred in it. The card is a parallelogram whose near edge is PARALLEL
    to the frame edge (constant gap, no straight-against-slanted wedge). Returns (cx, cy, w, h, shear) - shear = dx / dy of the
    card's vertical edges, cx / cy = centre of the card's middle row - or None when nothing fits."""
    if side == "left":                                   # mirror the world, solve for the right side, mirror back
        quads = [[(W - p[0], p[1]) for p in (q[1], q[0], q[3], q[2])] for q in quads]
    q0 = quads[0]
    s = (q0[2][0] - q0[1][0]) / ((q0[2][1] - q0[1][1]) or 1e-9)
    xr, yc = W - edge, (yt + yb) / 2.0

    def solve(w):
        h = w / ar
        if h > yb - yt + 1e-6:
            return None
        ys = [yc - h / 2.0 + h * i / 16.0 for i in range(17)]
        nl = max(_el_edge_x(q, y) + gap - s * (y - yc) for q in quads for y in ys)      # card's left edge at yc, all rows clear
        slack = xr - (nl + w + abs(s) * h / 2.0)
        return (nl, h, slack) if slack >= 0 else None
    hi = min(xr, (yb - yt) * ar)
    if cap_w:
        hi = min(hi, float(cap_w))
    if hi < 2.0:
        return None
    w, r = hi, solve(hi)
    if r is None:
        lo, best = 2.0, None
        for _ in range(40):
            mid = (lo + hi) / 2.0
            rr = solve(mid)
            if rr:
                best, lo = (mid, rr), mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        if best is None:
            return None
        w, r = best
    nl, h, slack = r
    cx = nl + w / 2.0 + slack / 2.0
    return (W - cx, yc, w, h, -s) if side == "left" else (cx, yc, w, h, s)


def _el_side_ctx(cfg, t3, start, end, duration):
    """Free-column context for a card alive in [start, end): (frame quads it must clear, top limit, bottom limit, end) in canvas px.
    Top = under the headline / tag lines, bottom = above the footer and above any strip / panel band already on screen when the card
    appears (the card keeps ONE size for its whole life). A band that starts moving in LATER than EL_SIDE_MIN_LIFE after the card
    appeared ends the card at that moment instead (it leaves just before the layout moves). Quad 0 = the full-size frame."""
    from mt.templates.t3.static import t3_frame_quad
    skew = 0.0 if t3["frame_style"] == "straight" else t3["frame_skew"]
    lay, base = cfg.get("_t3_lay"), _base(cfg)
    yb, quads = FOOT_TOP * K - 24.0, []
    if lay:
        quads.append(t3_frame_quad(_box(t3_layout("S0", base)["frame"]), skew))
        for k in "sp":
            for t0, t1, fa, fb in lay["tl"][k]:
                if fb > fa and t1 > start and start + EL_SIDE_MIN_LIFE <= t0 < end:
                    end = t0
        for a, b, sp in _segments(lay["tl"], lay["fps"], duration):
            if b <= start + 1e-6 or a >= end - 1e-6:
                continue
            L = t3_layout(sp, base)
            quads.append(t3_frame_quad(_box(L["frame"]), skew))
            for name in ("strip", "panel"):
                if L[name]:
                    yb = min(yb, L[name][1] - 20.0)
    else:
        quads.append(t3_frame_quad(cfg.get("video_box") or VIDEO_BOX, skew))
    if t3["show_headline"]:
        yt = (TAG_BOX[3] if any(t3["tag_lines"]) else UNDERLINE[2]) * K + 28.0
    else:
        yt = float(base["y"])
    return quads, yt, yb, end


def _el_shear(spr, s):
    """Horizontal shear x += s * y of an RGBA sprite (premultiplied, so no dark fringe); the output's centre = the card's centre."""
    if abs(s) < 1e-4:
        return spr
    w0, h0 = spr.size
    off = 0.0 if s >= 0 else -s * h0
    aff = getattr(Image, "Transform", Image).AFFINE
    out = spr.convert("RGBa").transform((w0 + int(math.ceil(abs(s) * h0)), h0), aff, (1.0, -s, -off, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0), Image.BICUBIC)
    return out.convert("RGBA")


def _el_side_place(cfg, t3, cfgx, label, ar, cap, start, end, duration):
    """Card geometry for place "side": ((cx, cy, w, h, shear), end) or None when there is no room at all. Falls back from
    side "left" to "right" when the left column is too small (the frame normally sits on the left), and warns when the card is small."""
    quads, yt, yb, end = _el_side_ctx(cfg, t3, start, end, duration)
    W = cfg["canvas"][0]
    g = _el_side_fit(quads, W, ar, cap, yt, yb, cfgx["side"])
    if (g is None or g[2] < EL_SIDE_MIN_W) and cfgx["side"] == "left":
        g2 = _el_side_fit(quads, W, ar, cap, yt, yb, "right")
        if g2 and (g is None or g2[2] > g[2]):
            t3_warn(f"   [!] element '{label}': no room on the left of the frame - placed on the right.")
            g = g2
    if g is None:
        t3_warn(f"   [!] element '{label}': no free column beside the frame - placed inside it.")
        return None
    if g[2] < EL_SIDE_MIN_W:
        t3_warn(f"   [!] element '{label}': the free column only fits a {int(g[2])} px wide card (small).")
    return g, end


def _el_side_items(cfg, e, duration, cfgx, im, t3, cap):
    """place "side": ONE static card in the free column beside the frame (see _el_side_fit); None = no room (caller falls back)."""
    start, end = float(e["start"]), min(float(e["end"]), duration + 1)
    if end - start <= 1e-6:
        return []
    for o in cfg.get("elements") or []:                  # the next card on the same side starts as this one ends -> overlap them
        ox = t3_el_cfg(o) if o is not e else None        # for the animation time: smooth swap instead of a hole
        try:
            if ox and ox["place"] == "side" and ox["side"] == cfgx["side"] and abs(float(o["start"]) - float(e["end"])) <= 0.15:
                end = min(end + float(e.get("anim_dur", 0.5)), duration + 1.0)
                break
        except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
            pass
    r = _el_side_place(cfg, t3, cfgx, Path(str(e.get("path"))).name, im.width / float(im.height), cap, start, end, duration)
    if r is None:
        return None
    (cx, cy, w, h, sh), end = r
    spr = _el_shear(t3_el_card_sprite(im, w, h, cfgx, t3["accent"]), sh)
    return [Item(spr, start, end, cx, cy, e.get("anim", "fade"), e.get("anim_dur", 0.5), out=True,
                 opacity=e.get("opacity", 100) / 100.0)]


# ---- video layers beside the frame (place "side": opt-in, same geometry / look as the image cards) --------------------------
def t3_split_side_video_layers(cfg):
    """(other video layers, side video layers): a T3 video layer is a card beside the frame only when it says place "side"
    (default = unchanged: over the frame box / the canvas, so existing b-roll jobs keep working)."""
    side, rest = [], []
    for L in cfg.get("video_layers") or []:
        (side if str(L.get("place") or "").strip().lower() == "side" else rest).append(L)
    return rest, side


def _el_cap(L, W):
    ws = str(L.get("width") or "").strip()
    try:
        return (float(ws[:-1]) / 100.0 * W if ws.endswith("%") else float(ws)) if ws else None
    except ValueError:
        return None


def t3_side_video_items(cfg, duration):
    """STILL preview of the side video layers: their first frame as an image card (renders use baked clips, t3_side_video_prepare)."""
    from mt.templates.t3.panels import _video_frame
    from mt.templates.t3.static import t3_cfg
    t3, out = t3_cfg(cfg.get("t3")), []
    for L in t3_split_side_video_layers(cfg)[1]:
        fr = _video_frame(L["path"], 0.0)
        cfgx = t3_el_cfg(dict(L, style="card", place="side"))
        if fr is None or cfgx is None:
            continue
        its = _el_side_items(cfg, dict(L, anim=L.get("anim", "fade")), duration, cfgx, fr.convert("RGBA"), t3, _el_cap(L, cfg["canvas"][0]))
        out += its or []
    return out


def t3_el_card_kit(w, h, cfgx, accent, shear=0.0):
    """The part of t3_el_card_sprite that does not depend on the picture (mask, border ring, shadow), computed once ->
    compose(rgb picture of exactly w x h) = the same sheared card sprite, about 10x faster (video cards: one call per frame)."""
    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageFilter
    from mt.draw import t2_rgb
    w, h = max(8, int(round(w))), max(8, int(round(h)))
    SS, r, pad = 4, min(cfgx["radius"], w / 2.0, h / 2.0), 30

    def rr(inset, rad):
        m = Image.new("L", (w * SS, h * SS), 0)
        ImageDraw.Draw(m).rounded_rectangle((inset * SS, inset * SS, (w - inset) * SS - 1, (h - inset) * SS - 1),
                                            radius=max(0.0, rad) * SS, fill=255)
        return m
    mask0 = rr(0, r).resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)
    mask = rr(1, r - 1).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(1.2 * SS)).resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)     # video frames have no alpha
    ring = None
    if cfgx["border"] > 0:
        bw = cfgx["border"]
        rg = ImageChops.subtract(rr(0, r), rr(bw, r - bw)).resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)
        ring = Image.new("RGBA", (w, h), tuple(t2_rgb(cfgx["color"] or accent)) + (255,))
        ring.putalpha(rg)
    shadow = Image.new("RGBA", (w + 2 * pad, h + 2 * pad), (0, 0, 0, 255))
    sh = Image.new("L", shadow.size, 0)
    sh.paste(mask0, (pad, pad + 6))
    shadow.putalpha(sh.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(12)).point(lambda v: int(v * 0.40)))

    def compose(img):
        img = img.convert("RGBA")
        img.putalpha(mask)
        if ring is not None:
            img.alpha_composite(ring)
        spr = shadow.copy()
        spr.alpha_composite(img, (pad, pad))
        return _el_shear(spr, shear)
    return compose


def t3_side_video_prepare(cfg, info, tmpdir):
    """Side video layers -> baked alpha clips (qtrle): every frame = the picture in the rounded / bordered / sheared card with the
    entry / exit animation, drawn on a small local canvas at a fixed canvas position (key "_xy", area "canvas" = above the frame
    plate). Same mechanism as the split clips / floating cards. [] when there are none (or in a still)."""
    import subprocess
    from mt.anim import anim_state
    from mt.common import FFMPEG
    from mt.draw import draw_item
    from mt.templates.t3.static import t3_cfg
    vls = [] if cfg.get("_t2_still") else list(cfg.get("_t3_side_vl") or [])
    if not vls:
        return []
    t3, (W, H), fps = t3_cfg(cfg.get("t3")), cfg["canvas"], info["fps"]
    fps_f = float(fps)
    fps_s = f"{fps.numerator}/{fps.denominator}" if fps.denominator != 1 else str(fps.numerator)
    dur_eff = min(info["duration"], float(cfg["preview"])) if cfg.get("preview") else info["duration"]
    layers = []
    for n, L in enumerate(vls):
        s0, e_true = float(L["start"]), float(L["end"])
        if s0 >= dur_eff or e_true <= s0:
            continue
        cfgx = t3_el_cfg(dict(L, style="card", place="side"))
        vi = probe(L["path"])
        r = _el_side_place(cfg, t3, cfgx, Path(str(L["path"])).name, vi["w"] / float(vi["h"]), _el_cap(L, W), s0, e_true, info["duration"])
        if r is None:
            continue
        (cx, cy, w, h, sh), e_true = r
        w, h = max(8, int(round(w))), max(8, int(round(h)))
        compose = t3_el_card_kit(w, h, cfgx, t3["accent"], sh)
        ref = compose(Image.new("RGB", (w, h), (0, 0, 0)))
        Wp, Hp = int(ref.width * 1.3) // 2 * 2 + 2, int(ref.height * 1.3) // 2 * 2 + 2       # room for the pop overshoot
        x0, y0 = int(round(cx - Wp / 2.0)), int(round(cy - Hp / 2.0))
        e = min(e_true, dur_eff)
        nfr = max(1, int(math.ceil((e - s0) * fps_f)))
        out = os.path.join(tmpdir, f"t3sidevl{n}.mov")
        rd = subprocess.Popen([FFMPEG, "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-stream_loop", "-1", "-i", L["path"], "-an", "-vf",
                               f"fps={fps_s},scale={w}:{h},setsar=1,format=rgb24", "-frames:v", str(nfr), "-f", "rawvideo",
                               "-pix_fmt", "rgb24", "pipe:1"], stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        wr = subprocess.Popen([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgba",
                               "-video_size", f"{Wp}x{Hp}", "-framerate", fps_s, "-i", "pipe:0", "-c:v", "qtrle", "-pix_fmt", "argb", out],
                              stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        print(f"Preparing side video card {n + 1} ({Path(L['path']).name}, {nfr} frames)...")
        anim, adur, last = L.get("anim", "fade"), L.get("anim_dur", 0.4), None
        try:
            for i in range(nfr):
                need, raw = w * h * 3, b""
                while len(raw) < need:
                    ch = rd.stdout.read(need - len(raw))
                    if not ch:
                        break
                    raw += ch
                fr = Image.frombytes("RGB", (w, h), raw) if len(raw) == need else (last or Image.new("RGB", (w, h), (24, 15, 12)))
                last = fr
                it = Item(compose(fr), s0, e_true, Wp / 2.0, Hp / 2.0, anim, adur, out=True, opacity=L.get("opacity", 100) / 100.0)
                cv = Image.new("RGBA", (Wp, Hp), (0, 0, 0, 0))
                draw_item(cv, it, anim_state(it.anim, it.progress(s0 + i / fps_f), W, H))
                wr.stdin.write(cv.tobytes())
            wr.stdin.close()
        except (BrokenPipeError, OSError):
            pass
        finally:
            rd.kill()
            rd.wait()
        err = wr.stderr.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
        if wr.wait() != 0 or not os.path.isfile(out):
            raise SystemExit("[!] Could not prepare the side video card:\n" + err[-800:])
        layers.append({"path": out, "start": s0, "end": e, "area": "canvas", "mode": "fit", "fade": 0.0, "_xy": [x0, y0]})
    return layers


def t3_element_items(cfg, e, duration, margin=24):
    """Template 3 `elements` entry with style "card" (default) -> [Item]; None for style / place "plain" (caller keeps the old path).
    place "side" (default): the card sits in the free column BESIDE the frame (_el_side_items), never over the speaker.
    place "inside": the card sits INSIDE the real frame quad (margin px from every edge), centred on it, never cropped (fit "contain"),
    and follows the frame when the adaptive layout moves it (same segment cut as t3_layout_items: only the first piece
    keeps the in-animation). e["width"] (px or %) is only a maximum width; e["pos"] is ignored for cards.
    Needs: t3_cfg / t3_frame_quad (static.py), load_image_sprite / t2_rgb (draw.py), _segments / t3_layout / _base / _box (here)."""
    cfgx = t3_el_cfg(e)
    if cfgx is None:
        return None
    from mt.draw import load_image_sprite
    from mt.templates.t3.static import t3_cfg, t3_frame_quad
    t3 = t3_cfg(cfg.get("t3"))
    skew = 0.0 if t3["frame_style"] == "straight" else t3["frame_skew"]
    W = cfg["canvas"][0]
    im = load_image_sprite(e["path"], None, W)
    ws = str(e.get("width") or "").strip()
    try:
        cap = (float(ws[:-1]) / 100.0 * W if ws.endswith("%") else float(ws)) if ws else None
    except ValueError:
        cap = None
    if cfgx["place"] == "side":                           # default: beside the frame, never over the speaker
        side_items = _el_side_items(cfg, e, duration, cfgx, im, t3, cap)
        if side_items is not None:
            return side_items
        cfgx = dict(cfgx, place="inside")                 # no room at all -> the old inside card
    lay = cfg.get("_t3_lay")
    if lay and not lay.get("still"):
        base = _base(cfg)
        segs = [(a, b, _box(t3_layout(sp, base)["frame"])) for a, b, sp in _segments(lay["tl"], lay["fps"], duration)]
    else:
        segs = [(0.0, duration + 1.0, cfg.get("video_box") or VIDEO_BOX)]
    start, end = float(e["start"]), min(float(e["end"]), duration + 1)
    pieces = []
    for a, b, box in segs:
        ns, ne = max(start, a), min(end, b)
        if ne - ns <= 1e-6:
            continue
        qi = _el_inset_quad(t3_frame_quad(box, skew), margin)
        if cfgx["fit"] == "cover":
            qx, qy = [p[0] for p in qi], [p[1] for p in qi]
            ar = (max(qx) - min(qx)) / max(1.0, max(qy) - min(qy))
        else:
            ar = im.width / float(im.height)
        g = _el_fit_rect(qi, ar, cap)
        if g is None:
            continue
        key = tuple(int(round(v)) for v in g)
        if pieces and pieces[-1][3] == key and abs(pieces[-1][1] - ns) < 1e-6:
            pieces[-1][1] = ne
        else:
            pieces.append([ns, ne, g, key])
    cache, out = {}, []
    for k, (ns, ne, g, key) in enumerate(pieces):
        sk = key[2:]
        if sk not in cache:
            cache[sk] = t3_el_card_sprite(im, g[2], g[3], cfgx, t3["accent"])
        first, last = ns <= start + 1e-6, k == len(pieces) - 1
        out.append(Item(cache[sk], ns, ne, g[0], g[1], e.get("anim", "fade") if first else "none", e.get("anim_dur", 0.5),
                        out=(first and last), opacity=e.get("opacity", 100) / 100.0))
    return out


# ---- plates ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _right_frame(plate, bg_rgba, t3, rbox, x, margin=40):
    """S4: the matching (skewed, cream border, orange bracket) frame on the right, alpha = split progress x; its inside is a dark
    panel (the content - image / baked video - is drawn above by Items / video layers)."""
    W, H = bg_rgba.size
    rx, ry, rw, rh = [int(round(v)) for v in rbox]
    c0, r0, c1, r1 = max(0, rx - margin), max(0, ry - margin), min(W, rx + rw + margin), min(H, ry + rh + margin)
    crop = bg_rgba.crop((c0, r0, c1, r1)).convert("RGB")
    lay, info = t3_frame_layer(c1 - c0, r1 - r0, crop, (rx - c0, ry - r0, rw, rh), t3["frame_style"], t3["frame_skew"], t3["accent"],
                               full=True)
    dark = Image.new("RGBA", lay.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    ImageDraw.Draw(dark).polygon([tuple(p) for p in info["quad"]], fill=(24, 15, 12, 255))
    dark.alpha_composite(lay)
    if x < 0.999:
        dark.putalpha(dark.getchannel("A").point(lambda v, a=x: int(v * a)))
    plate.alpha_composite(dark, (c0, r0))


def _plate(bg_rgba, top, t3, frame, margin=64, right=None, x=0.0):
    """Full-canvas plate: opaque design everywhere except the (transparent) video window + border/shadow/bracket of `frame`.
    right/x (T3-9): the S4 right frame box and the split progress."""
    W, H = bg_rgba.size
    x, y, w, h = [int(round(v)) for v in frame]
    c0, r0, c1, r1 = max(0, x - margin), max(0, y - margin), min(W, x + w + margin), min(H, y + h + margin)
    crop = bg_rgba.crop((c0, r0, c1, r1)).convert("RGB")
    lay, _ = t3_frame_layer(c1 - c0, r1 - r0, crop, (x - c0, y - r0, w, h), t3["frame_style"], t3["frame_skew"],
                            t3["accent"], full=True)
    plate = bg_rgba.copy()
    plate.paste(lay, (c0, r0))
    if right is not None and x > 1e-3:
        _right_frame(plate, bg_rgba, t3, right, x)
    plate.alpha_composite(top)
    return plate


def _band_layer(cfg, sp, t_progress=None):
    """Bands of presence sp as an RGBA canvas layer (still previews only; renders overlay them with ffmpeg)."""
    W, H = cfg["canvas"]
    lay = t3_layout(sp, _base(cfg))
    out = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    for name, pres in (("strip", sp[0]), ("panel", sp[1])):
        r = lay[name]
        if r:
            im = _band_image(cfg, name, int(round(r[2])), int(round(r[3])))
            im.putalpha(im.getchannel("A").point(lambda v, a=pres: int(v * a)))
            out.alpha_composite(im, (int(round(r[0])), int(round(r[1] + (1 - pres) * SLIDE * K))))
    return out


# ---- ffmpeg expressions ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _expr(tr, v0):
    """ffmpeg expression in t for a presence track (smoothstep transitions)."""
    if not tr:
        return "%g" % v0
    e = "%g" % tr[-1][3]
    for t0, t1, a, b in reversed(tr):
        u = "((t-%.4f)/%.4f)" % (t0, t1 - t0)
        e = "if(lt(t,%.4f),%g,if(lt(t,%.4f),%g+(%g)*(%s*%s*(3-2*%s)),%s))" % (t0, a, t1, a, b - a, u, u, u, e)
    return e


def _pexprs(tl):
    return _expr(tl["s"], tl["v0"][0]), _expr(tl["p"], tl["v0"][1])


def _hexpr(S, P, y0, dims=None):
    sh, gt, gb = dims or (STRIP_H, GAP, 0.0)
    return "(%.3f-%.3f*(%s)-(%.3f)*(%s)+(%.3f)*(%s)*(%s)-%g)" % (
        (FOOT_TOP - GAP) * K, PANEL_H * K, P, (sh + gt + gb - GAP) * K, S, (gb - GAP) * K, P, S, y0)


# ---- prepare (hook, called from t3_build_static_layers) ----------------------------------------------------------------
def t3_layout_prepare(cfg, tmp, bg_img, fg_img):
    """Builds everything the layout needs into cfg: cfg['video_box'] = frame at the start (items anchor to it), and for a render
    cfg['_t3_lay'] (plates, band PNGs, ffmpeg expressions; consumed by build_ffmpeg_cmd via t3_layout_inputs/graph/video).
    A --still previews the layout state at the still's time t (plate + bands baked into the foreground plate)."""
    t3, W, H = cfg["t3"], cfg["canvas"][0], cfg["canvas"][1]
    vb = dict(cfg.get("video_box") or VIDEO_BOX)
    base = {"x": vb["x"], "y": vb["y"], "w": vb["w"]}
    from mt.templates.t3.panels import t3_strip_geom                    # strip height / gaps from the free area (only when strip.on)
    base.update(t3_strip_geom(cfg, base))
    cfg["_t3_base"] = dict(base)
    mode = t3["video_fit"]
    vid = cfg.get("video")
    info = probe(vid) if vid and os.path.isfile(str(vid)) else None
    fps = float(info["fps"]) if info else 30.0
    duration = float(info["duration"]) if info else 5.0
    if cfg.get("preview"):
        duration = min(duration, float(cfg["preview"]))
    tl = t3_layout_timeline(cfg, duration)
    head = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    t3_headline_layer(head, t3)
    _tops = {}

    def top_for(x):                                                   # headline column fades out while the split is on
        k = round(x * 1000)
        if k not in _tops:
            t_ = head.copy()
            if k:
                t_.putalpha(t_.getchannel("A").point(lambda v, a=1 - x: int(v * a)))
            t_.alpha_composite(fg_img)
            _tops[k] = t_
        return _tops[k]
    bg_rgba = bg_img.convert("RGBA")
    pk = lambda sp: "%d_%d_%d" % (round(sp[0] * 1000), round(sp[1] * 1000), round((sp[2] if len(sp) > 2 else 0) * 1000))
    mkplate = lambda sp: _plate(bg_rgba, top_for(sp[2]), t3, t3_layout(sp, base)["frame"], right=t3_layout(sp, base)["right"], x=sp[2])
    if cfg.get("_t2_still"):
        sp = _spx_at(tl, float(cfg.get("_t3_still_t", 0.5)))
        fr = t3_layout(sp, base)["frame"]
        plate = mkplate(sp)
        plate.alpha_composite(_band_layer(dict(cfg, video_box=base), sp))
        path = os.path.join(tmp, "t3_lay_still.png")
        plate.save(path, compress_level=1)
        cfg["_t2_fg"] = path
        cfg["video_box"] = dict({k: int(round(v)) for k, v in _box(fr).items()}, mode=mode)
        cfg["_t3_lay"] = {"tl": tl, "fps": fps, "still": True}
        return
    nfr = int(math.ceil(duration * fps))
    wins = _windows(tl, fps, nfr)
    half = 0.5 / fps
    const, bounds, cur = {}, [], 0
    for i0, i1 in wins + [(nfr + 1, nfr + 1)]:                       # constant stretches between the windows
        a, b = cur / fps, (9999.0 if i0 > nfr else i0 / fps)
        if b > a + 1e-9:
            sp = _spx_at(tl, a)
            const.setdefault(pk(sp), {"sp": sp, "en": []})["en"].append((a, b))
        cur = i1
    lay = {"tl": tl, "fps": fps, "const": [], "windows": [], "bands": []}
    for k, c in const.items():
        path = os.path.join(tmp, f"t3_plate_{k}.png")
        mkplate(c["sp"]).save(path, compress_level=1)
        lay["const"].append({"path": path, "en": "+".join("gte(t,%.4f)*lt(t,%.4f)" % (a - half, b - half) for a, b in c["en"])})
    for wi, (i0, i1) in enumerate(wins):
        for i in range(i0, i1):
            mkplate(_spx_at(tl, i / fps)).save(os.path.join(tmp, f"t3_tr{wi}_{i - i0:04d}.png"), compress_level=1)
        lay["windows"].append({"pattern": os.path.join(tmp, f"t3_tr{wi}_%04d.png"), "t0": i0 / fps, "t1": i1 / fps})
    S, P = _pexprs(tl)
    for name, track, X in (("strip", "s", S), ("panel", "p", P)):
        ivs, st = [], ((0.0, 0.0) if tl["v0"][0 if track == "s" else 1] == 1.0 else None)
        for t0, t1, a, b in tl[track]:
            if b > a:
                st = (t0, t1 - t0)
            elif st is not None:
                ivs.append((st[0], st[1], t0, t1 - t0, t1))
                st = None
        if st is not None:
            ivs.append((st[0], st[1], None, 0, duration + 1.0))
        if not ivs:
            continue
        r = t3_layout(STATES["S3"], base)[name]
        w, h = int(round(r[2])), int(round(r[3]))
        path = os.path.join(tmp, f"t3_band_{name}.png")
        _band_image(cfg, name, w, h).save(path)
        ybase = ("%.3f-%.3f*(%s)" % (r[1], (PANEL_H + GAP) * K, P)) if name == "strip" else "%.3f" % r[1]
        lay["bands"].append({"name": name, "path": path, "x": r[0], "y": "%s+(1-(%s))*%.2f" % (ybase, X, SLIDE * K), "ivs": ivs})
    y0 = float(base["y"])
    H_t = _hexpr(S, P, y0, _strip_dims(base))
    bx, bw = float(base["x"]), float(base["w"])
    bwx = "%g" % bw if not tl.get("x") else "(%g*(1-%g*(%s)))" % (bw, 1 - SPLIT_LEFT, _expr(tl["x"], 0.0))   # S4: frame width expr
    if mode == "fill":
        vw = "trunc(max(%s,%s*iw/ih)/2)*2" % (bwx, H_t)
        vh = "trunc(max(%s,%s*ih/iw)/2)*2" % (H_t, bwx)
    else:
        vw = "trunc(min(%s,%s*iw/ih)/2)*2" % (bwx, H_t)
        vh = "trunc(min(%s,%s*ih/iw)/2)*2" % (H_t, bwx)
    lay["video"] = {"vf": "scale=w='%s':h='%s':eval=frame:flags=bilinear,setsar=1" % (vw, vh),
                    "ox": "'%g+(%s-overlay_w)/2'" % (bx, bwx), "oy": "'%g+(%s-overlay_h)/2'" % (y0, H_t)}
    cfg["_t3_lay"] = lay
    cfg.pop("_t2_fg", None)                                            # the layout plates replace the single fixed plate
    cfg["video_box"] = dict({k: int(round(v)) for k, v in _box(t3_layout(_spx_at(tl, 0.0), base)["frame"]).items()}, mode=mode)


def t3_layout_video(cfg):
    """(video filter chain, overlay x expr, overlay y expr) replacing the static scale/crop in build_ffmpeg_cmd."""
    v = cfg["_t3_lay"]["video"]
    return v["vf"], v["ox"], v["oy"]


def t3_layout_inputs(cfg, fps_s, duration):
    """Extra ffmpeg input arguments (plates, transition sequences, band PNGs) in the order t3_layout_graph expects."""
    lay, a = cfg["_t3_lay"], []
    for c in lay["const"]:
        a += ["-loop", "1", "-framerate", fps_s, "-t", f"{duration:.3f}", "-i", c["path"]]
    for w in lay["windows"]:
        a += ["-framerate", fps_s, "-itsoffset", f"{w['t0']:.5f}", "-i", w["pattern"]]
    for b in lay["bands"]:
        a += ["-loop", "1", "-framerate", fps_s, "-t", f"{duration:.3f}", "-i", b["path"]]
    return a


def t3_layout_graph(cfg, cur, idx0):
    """Filter-graph fragment: plates (state / transition frames) then the bands. Returns (fragment, new current label)."""
    lay, fc, i = cfg["_t3_lay"], "", idx0
    half = 0.5 / lay["fps"]
    for j, c in enumerate(lay["const"]):
        fc += f";[{cur}][{i}:v]overlay=0:0:eof_action=pass:format=auto:enable='{c['en']}'[t3pl{j}]"
        cur, i = f"t3pl{j}", i + 1
    for j, w in enumerate(lay["windows"]):
        fc += (f";[{cur}][{i}:v]overlay=0:0:eof_action=pass:format=auto:"
               f"enable='gte(t,{w['t0'] - half:.4f})*lt(t,{w['t1'] - half:.4f})'[t3pw{j}]")
        cur, i = f"t3pw{j}", i + 1
    for j, b in enumerate(lay["bands"]):
        n = len(b["ivs"])
        fc += f";[{i}:v]format=yuva420p,split={n}" + "".join(f"[t3b{j}_{k}]" for k in range(n)) if n > 1 else \
            f";[{i}:v]format=yuva420p[t3b{j}_0]"
        for k, (u0, ud, d0, dd, end) in enumerate(b["ivs"]):
            fd = f"fade=t=in:st={u0:.4f}:d={max(ud, 0.05):.4f}:alpha=1" if ud > 0 else "null"
            if d0 is not None:
                fd += f",fade=t=out:st={d0:.4f}:d={max(dd, 0.05):.4f}:alpha=1"
            fc += (f";[t3b{j}_{k}]{fd}[t3bf{j}_{k}];[{cur}][t3bf{j}_{k}]overlay=x={b['x']:.2f}:y='{b['y']}':eof_action=pass:"
                   f"format=auto:enable='between(t,{u0:.4f},{end:.4f})'[t3bo{j}_{k}]")
            cur = f"t3bo{j}_{k}"
        i += 1
    return fc, cur


def t3_layout_input_count(cfg):
    lay = cfg["_t3_lay"]
    return len(lay["const"]) + len(lay["windows"]) + len(lay["bands"])


# ---- S4 right-frame content (T3-9) -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _seg_times(seg, tr):
    """(start, end) of a segment's content: the first one fades in with the x track, the last one fades out with it."""
    return seg["a"], seg["b"] + (tr if seg["last"] else 0.0)


def _is_video(path):
    return str(path).lower().endswith(VIDEO_EXTS)


def _right_geom(cfg):
    """(box (x, y, w, h) ints, quad local to the box) of the right frame at S4."""
    r = t3_layout("S4", _base(cfg))["right"]
    box = tuple(int(round(v)) for v in r)
    from mt.templates.t3.static import t3_frame_quad
    t3 = cfg["t3"]
    quad = t3_frame_quad((0, 0, box[2], box[3]), 0.0 if t3["frame_style"] == "straight" else t3["frame_skew"])
    return box, quad


def _quad_mask(w, h, quad, inset=2):
    ss = 3
    m = Image.new("L", (w * ss, h * ss), 0)
    cx, cy = w / 2.0, h / 2.0
    pts = []
    for px, py in quad:                                               # shrink the quad a little: the cream border covers the edge
        d = math.hypot(px - cx, py - cy) or 1.0
        pts.append(((px - (px - cx) / d * inset) * ss, (py - (py - cy) / d * inset) * ss))
    ImageDraw.Draw(m).polygon(pts, fill=255)
    return m.resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)


def _fit_photo(photo, w, h, mode):
    photo = photo.convert("RGB")
    if mode == "fit":
        sc = min(w / photo.width, h / photo.height)
        im = photo.resize((max(1, int(photo.width * sc)), max(1, int(photo.height * sc))), Image.LANCZOS)
        out = Image.new("RGB", (w, h), (24, 15, 12))
        out.paste(im, ((w - im.width) // 2, (h - im.height) // 2))
        return out
    sc = max(w / photo.width, h / photo.height)
    im = photo.resize((max(w, int(round(photo.width * sc))), max(h, int(round(photo.height * sc)))), Image.LANCZOS)
    l, t = (im.width - w) // 2, (im.height - h) // 2
    return im.crop((l, t, l + w, t + h))


def split_sprite(cfg, seg, photo=None):
    """RGBA sprite (right frame box size) of a segment: image / video frame / original-video frame, quad-masked."""
    box, quad = _right_geom(cfg)
    w, h = box[2], box[3]
    if photo is None:
        from mt.templates.t3 import panels as _pn
        photo = None
        if seg["media"] and not _is_video(seg["media"]):
            try:
                photo = Image.open(seg["media"]).convert("RGB")
                photo.load()
            except Exception:
                t3_warn(f"   [!] split image unreadable: {seg['media']}")
        elif cfg.get("video") or seg["media"]:
            photo = _pn._video_frame(seg["media"] or cfg["video"], 0.0 if seg["media"] else seg["a"])
        photo = photo or Image.new("RGB", (64, 64), (96, 96, 96))
    sp = _fit_photo(photo, w, h, seg["mode"]).convert("RGBA")
    sp.putalpha(_quad_mask(w, h, quad))
    return sp


def split_items(cfg, duration):
    """Right-frame content Items (images, or a frame of video media in a still); videos are baked layers (split_prepare_layers)."""
    segs = split_resolve(cfg, duration)
    if not segs:
        return []
    from mt.templates.t3 import dynamic as _dyn
    tr = float(cfg["t3"]["layout"]["trans"])
    box, _ = _right_geom(cfg)
    still = bool(cfg.get("_t2_still"))
    out = []
    for g in segs:
        if _is_video(g["media"]) and not still and os.path.isfile(g["media"]):
            continue
        a, b = _seg_times(g, tr)
        if not _dyn._vis(a, b):
            continue
        out.append(Item(split_sprite(cfg, g), a, b, box[0] + box[2] / 2.0, box[1] + box[3] / 2.0, "fade", tr, out=True))
    return out


def split_prepare_layers(cfg, info, tmpdir):
    """Video media of split segments -> alpha clips (quad mask + fade baked), same mechanism as the floating cards / slot media."""
    import subprocess
    from mt.anim import anim_state
    from mt.common import FFMPEG
    from mt.draw import draw_item
    if cfg.get("_t2_still") or not (cfg.get("t3") or {}).get("split", {}).get("on"):
        return []
    segs = [g for g in split_resolve(cfg, info["duration"]) if _is_video(g["media"]) and os.path.isfile(g["media"])]
    if not segs:
        return []
    W, H = cfg["canvas"]
    fps = info["fps"]
    fps_f = float(fps)
    fps_s = f"{fps.numerator}/{fps.denominator}" if fps.denominator != 1 else str(fps.numerator)
    dur_eff = min(info["duration"], float(cfg["preview"])) if cfg.get("preview") else info["duration"]
    tr = float(cfg["t3"]["layout"]["trans"])
    box, quad = _right_geom(cfg)
    w, h = box[0:4][2], box[3]
    mask = _quad_mask(w, h, quad)
    Wp, Hp = w - w % 2, h - h % 2
    layers = []
    for n, g in enumerate(segs):
        t0, t1 = _seg_times(g, tr)
        if t0 >= dur_eff:
            continue
        e = min(t1, dur_eff)
        nfr = max(1, int(math.ceil((e - t0) * fps_f)))
        out = os.path.join(tmpdir, f"t3split{n}.mov")
        vf = f"fps={fps_s},scale={w}:{h}:force_original_aspect_ratio={'decrease' if g['mode'] == 'fit' else 'increase'}"
        vf += f",pad={w}:{h}:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2:color=0x180f0c,format=rgb24" if g["mode"] == "fit" else f",crop={w}:{h},format=rgb24"
        rd = subprocess.Popen([FFMPEG, "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-stream_loop", "-1", "-i", g["media"], "-an", "-vf", vf,
                               "-frames:v", str(nfr), "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgb24", "pipe:1"],
                              stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        wr = subprocess.Popen([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgba",
                               "-video_size", f"{Wp}x{Hp}", "-framerate", fps_s, "-i", "pipe:0", "-c:v", "qtrle", "-pix_fmt", "argb", out],
                              stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        print(f"Preparing split clip {n + 1} ({Path(g['media']).name}, {nfr} frames)...")
        last = None
        try:
            for i in range(nfr):
                need = w * h * 3
                raw = b""
                while len(raw) < need:
                    ch = rd.stdout.read(need - len(raw))
                    if not ch:
                        break
                    raw += ch
                fr = Image.frombytes("RGB", (w, h), raw) if len(raw) == need else last
                fr = fr or Image.new("RGB", (w, h), (24, 15, 12))
                last = fr
                spr = fr.convert("RGBA")
                spr.putalpha(mask)
                it = Item(spr, t0, t1, w / 2.0, h / 2.0, "fade", tr, out=True)
                cv = Image.new("RGBA", (w, h), (0, 0, 0, 0))
                draw_item(cv, it, anim_state(it.anim, it.progress(t0 + i / fps_f), W, H))
                wr.stdin.write(cv.crop((0, 0, Wp, Hp)).tobytes())
            wr.stdin.close()
        except (BrokenPipeError, OSError):
            pass
        finally:
            rd.kill()
            rd.wait()
        err = wr.stderr.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
        if wr.wait() != 0 or not os.path.isfile(out):
            raise SystemExit("[!] Could not prepare the split clip:\n" + err[-800:])
        layers.append({"path": out, "start": t0, "end": e, "area": "canvas", "mode": "fit", "fade": 0.0, "_xy": [box[0], box[1]]})
    return layers


def t3_ask_split():
    """Split-screen questions (Enter = default / last answer). Returns the t3 keys to merge ({} = no split)."""
    from mt.common import D, ask, ask_choice, yn
    from mt.templates.t3.dynamic import _c_start
    on = yn("\nAdd split-screen moments (the video shrinks to the left, an image / video appears on the right)?",
            default=D.get("t3_split", "n") == "y")
    D["t3_split"] = "y" if on else "n"
    if not on:
        return {}
    segs = []
    while len(segs) < 8:
        k = len(segs)
        print(f"--- Split moment #{k + 1} ---")
        media = ask("  Right-side image or video file ('-' = a frame of your own video)", default="-", key=f"t3_split_media{k}",
                    cast=lambda v: "" if v.strip() in ("", "-") else v.strip().strip('"'))
        mode = ("fill", "fit")[ask_choice("  Fit inside the right frame:", ["Fill (crop)", "Fit (whole picture)"], default=1,
                                          key=f"t3_split_mode{k}") - 1]
        kind, val = ask("  Starts at (seconds / mm:ss / spoken phrase)", default="2" if k == 0 else None, key=f"t3_split_st{k}",
                        cast=_c_start)
        hold = ask("  Stays (seconds)", default=4, key=f"t3_split_hold{k}", cast=float)
        segs.append({"start": val if kind == "t" else None, "trigger": val if kind == "p" else "", "hold": hold, "media": media,
                     "mode": mode})
        if not yn("Add another split moment?", default=False):
            break
    return {"split": {"on": True, "segments": segs}, "layout": {"on": True, "mode": "auto"}}



========================================================================================
FILE: mt\anim.py
========================================================================================
"""mt.anim - moved from montage.py by tools/mt_move.py"""


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Animations
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
ANIMS = ["fade", "slide_up", "slide_down", "slide_left", "slide_right",
         "zoom_in", "pop", "spin_in", "reveal", "drop"]


def _out_cubic(p):
    return 1 - (1 - p) ** 3


def _out_back(p, s=1.70158):
    p -= 1
    return 1 + (s + 1) * p ** 3 + s * p ** 2


def _out_bounce(p):
    n1, d1 = 7.5625, 2.75
    if p < 1 / d1:
        return n1 * p * p
    if p < 2 / d1:
        p -= 1.5 / d1
        return n1 * p * p + 0.75
    if p < 2.5 / d1:
        p -= 2.25 / d1
        return n1 * p * p + 0.9375
    p -= 2.625 / d1
    return n1 * p * p + 0.984375


def anim_state(name, p, W, H):
    """p: 0 (hidden) -> 1 (fully shown). Returns alpha, dx, dy, scale, rotation, reveal."""
    if name == "t4_pop":                        # T4 tiles (t4/tiles.py): in = pop (overshoot + fade); out arrives as p < 0 = pure fade of -p
        return 1.0 if p >= 1 else (min(1.0, -p) if p < 0 else min(1.0, p * 4)), 0, 0, 1.0 if (p >= 1 or p < 0) else max(0.01, _out_back(p)), 0.0, None
    if name in (None, "none") or p >= 1:
        return 1.0, 0, 0, 1.0, 0.0, None
    p = max(0.0, p)
    e = _out_cubic(p)
    if name == "fade":
        return e, 0, 0, 1.0, 0.0, None
    if name == "slide_up":
        return e, 0, (1 - e) * 0.10 * H, 1.0, 0.0, None
    if name == "slide_down":
        return e, 0, -(1 - e) * 0.10 * H, 1.0, 0.0, None
    if name == "slide_left":
        return e, (1 - e) * 0.18 * W, 0, 1.0, 0.0, None
    if name == "slide_right":
        return e, -(1 - e) * 0.18 * W, 0, 1.0, 0.0, None
    if name == "zoom_in":
        return min(1.0, p * 2), 0, 0, 0.4 + 0.6 * e, 0.0, None
    if name == "pop":
        return min(1.0, p * 4), 0, 0, max(0.01, _out_back(p)), 0.0, None
    if name == "spin_in":
        return e, 0, 0, max(0.01, e), (1 - e) * 270.0, None
    if name == "reveal":
        return 1.0, 0, 0, 1.0, 0.0, e
    if name == "drop":
        return min(1.0, p * 5), 0, -(1 - _out_bounce(p)) * 0.22 * H, 1.0, 0.0, None
    if name == "tile_up":                       # T3-6: short slide-up (60 px) + fade for the photo tiles; reversed = slide down + fade
        return min(1.0, p * 2.5), 0, (1 - e) * 60.0, 1.0, 0.0, None
    if name in ("card_r", "card_l"):
        # Template 2 floating card: slides in from the right / left edge with a small rotation settle
        # (ease-out-back); the same curve run backwards = slide + fade out.
        s = 1.0 if name == "card_r" else -1.0
        b = _out_back(p, 1.1)
        return min(1.0, p * 3.5), s * (1 - b) * 0.20 * W, 0, 1.0, s * (1 - b) * 10.0, None
    return 1.0, 0, 0, 1.0, 0.0, None


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Positions
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
PRESETS = {
    "1": "top-left", "2": "top", "3": "top-right",
    "4": "left", "5": "center", "6": "right",
    "7": "bottom-left", "8": "bottom", "9": "bottom-right",
}



========================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\cfg.py
========================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.cfg - Template 4 "Overlay Studio": config defaults + normaliser (cfg["t4"]).
The user's video is the BASE and fills the canvas; every module below is an overlay layer (placeholders until their P-task)."""
import copy
import re

CANVAS_REELS = [1080, 1920]
CANVAS_YOUTUBE = [1920, 1080]
T4_MODULES = ("frame", "nameplate", "corner_list", "tags", "tiles", "cards", "scenes", "split", "broll")
T4_DEFAULTS = {
    "canvas_mode": "auto",                                   # 'auto' (by the video aspect) | 'reels' | 'youtube'
    "fit": "fill",                                           # 'fill' (crop to the canvas) | 'fit' (whole video, letterboxed)
    "backdrop": {"type": "color", "value": "#000000", "path": "", "strength": 5},   # type color|image|blur; only visible when fit leaves bars
    **{m: {"on": False} for m in T4_MODULES},
}
_HEX = re.compile(r"^#?([0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{3})$")


def t4_canvas(mode, video_info=None):
    """-> [1080, 1920] (reels) or [1920, 1080] (youtube). 'auto' = by the video aspect (wider than tall -> youtube; no info -> reels)."""
    m = str(mode or "auto").strip().lower()
    if m == "youtube":
        return list(CANVAS_YOUTUBE)
    if m == "reels":
        return list(CANVAS_REELS)
    try:
        return list(CANVAS_YOUTUBE) if float(video_info["w"]) > float(video_info["h"]) else list(CANVAS_REELS)
    except (TypeError, KeyError, ValueError):
        return list(CANVAS_REELS)


def _hex(v, default):
    m = _HEX.match(str(v).strip()) if isinstance(v, str) else None
    if not m:
        return default
    h = m.group(1)
    h = "".join(c * 2 for c in h) if len(h) == 3 else h
    return "#" + h.lower()


def _choice(v, allowed, default):
    v = str(v).strip().lower() if isinstance(v, str) else ""
    return v if v in allowed else default


def t4_cfg(raw):
    """Normalise cfg["t4"]: always returns a complete dict, never raises (junk values fall back to the defaults).
    Module dicts keep their extra keys (later P-tasks own them); only `on` is coerced to bool."""
    out = copy.deepcopy(T4_DEFAULTS)
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return out
    try:
        out["canvas_mode"] = _choice(raw.get("canvas_mode"), ("auto", "reels", "youtube"), out["canvas_mode"])
        out["fit"] = _choice(raw.get("fit"), ("fill", "fit"), out["fit"])
        bd = raw.get("backdrop")
        if isinstance(bd, dict):
            b = out["backdrop"]
            b["type"] = _choice(bd.get("type"), ("color", "image", "blur"), b["type"])
            b["value"] = _hex(bd.get("value"), b["value"])
            b["path"] = bd["path"].strip() if isinstance(bd.get("path"), str) else ""
            try:
                b["strength"] = max(1, min(10, int(bd.get("strength", b["strength"]))))
            except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
                pass
        for m in T4_MODULES:
            src = raw.get(m)
            if isinstance(src, dict):
                d = copy.deepcopy(src)
                d["on"] = bool(src.get("on", False))
                out[m] = d
            elif m in ("tiles", "cards", "scenes") and isinstance(src, list):          # tiles / cards = a LIST (t4/tiles.py, t4/cards.py normalise it)
                out[m] = copy.deepcopy(src)
        for k, v in raw.items():                         # unknown keys survive (forward compatibility with newer jobs)
            if k not in out:
                out[k] = copy.deepcopy(v)
    except Exception:
        return copy.deepcopy(T4_DEFAULTS)
    return out



========================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\split.py
========================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.split - Template 4 module 'split' (ref R7): diagonal split-screen takeover. The speaker video stays untouched underneath;
a media clip (video or image) shows through a diagonal polygon on one side, with a glowing neon seam on the diagonal and two HUD corner labels
('01 - TALKING HEAD' / '02 - LIVE DEVELOPMENT') with corner brackets. Entry wipe = the seam sweeps along its own normal from the media-side edge
to its place, exit wipe = the same path back.
cfg['t4']['split'] = {on (REQUIRED true in a job file), media 'path' (image or video; missing -> gray placeholder + one warning),
    angle 12 (lean of the seam in degrees, -35..35; + = '/' ), side 'auto'|'bottom'|'top'|'left'|'right' (auto = bottom on reels, right on YouTube),
    pos (seam position, fraction of the canvas along the split axis; default 0.5 top/bottom, 0.6 right, 0.4 left),
    labels [text | {text, color, anchor}, ...] (2 entries; '-' or '' hides one), color '#19d3ff', seam_width 3, wipe 0.55 s,
    seg_start / seg_end (video media: plays from seg_start; without seg_end it loops, otherwise plays once and holds the last frame),
    start | trigger, end | hold (default 6 s)}
Drawing split: the media picture is BAKED by split_video.py into an alpha clip (area 'box' = under the frame plate); the seam (one tiny Item per
frame while it moves) and the labels are ordinary Items. In --still the media is drawn as an Item (first frame).
Public: T4_SPLIT, t4_split_cfg(raw), t4_split_plan(cfg, duration, fps), t4_split_u(plan, t), t4_split_mask(plan, u), t4_split_cover(img, size),
t4_split_items(cfg, duration), t4_ask_split()."""
import copy
import math
import os
import re

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageOps

from mt.common import IMAGE_EXTS, VIDEO_EXTS, ask, ask_choice, c_color, c_existing_file, c_float, probe, yn
from mt.draw import Item, t2_lighten, t2_paint, t2_rgb, t2_text_mask
from mt.fonts import has_arabic, is_rtl_text
from mt.templates.t4.cfg import _hex
from mt.templates.t4.frame import t4_frame_cfg
from mt.templates.t4.kit import connector, glass_panel, kit_paste
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import _anchor as _corner, _bool, _flt, _font, _frame_mask, _place, _window
from mt.templates.t4.timing import _num as _secs, t4_ask_when
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

SIDES = ("top", "bottom", "left", "right")
POS_DEFAULT = {"top": 0.5, "bottom": 0.5, "left": 0.4, "right": 0.6}
DEFAULT_LABELS = ("01 \u2014 TALKING HEAD", "02 \u2014 LIVE DEVELOPMENT")
LABEL_ANCHORS = {"bottom": ("top-left", "bottom-left"), "top": ("bottom-left", "top-left"),      # (speaker label, media label)
                 "right": ("top-left", "top-right"), "left": ("top-right", "top-left")}
TINT = (5, 12, 30, 214)
T4_SPLIT = {"on": False, "media": "", "angle": 12.0, "side": "auto", "pos": None, "labels": [], "color": "#19d3ff", "seam_width": 3.0,
            "wipe": 0.55, "seg_start": None, "seg_end": None, "start": 0.0, "trigger": "", "end": None, "hold": None}
_NUMRE = re.compile(r"^\s*(\d{1,3})\s*[\u2014\u2013\-]+\s*(.+?)\s*$")


# ---- config -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _labels(raw):
    """-> exactly 2 dicts {text, color, anchor}; missing -> the R7 texts; '-' / '' / 'none' -> text '' (hidden)."""
    src = raw if isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)) else []
    out = []
    for i in range(2):
        r = src[i] if i < len(src) else None
        d = r if isinstance(r, dict) else {"text": r}
        t = d.get("text")
        t = DEFAULT_LABELS[i] if (t is None or isinstance(t, bool)) else str(t).strip()[:60]
        if t.lower() in ("-", "none"):
            t = ""
        c = d.get("color")
        out.append({"text": t, "color": _hex(c, "") if isinstance(c, str) else "", "anchor": _corner(d.get("anchor"), "")})
    return out


def t4_split_cfg(raw):
    """Normalise cfg['t4']['split'] -> complete dict (extras survive). Never raises; junk -> defaults (on False)."""
    out = copy.deepcopy(T4_SPLIT)
    out["labels"] = _labels(None)
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return out
    try:
        out.update({k: copy.deepcopy(v) for k, v in raw.items() if k not in out})
        m = raw.get("media")
        out["media"] = m.strip() if isinstance(m, str) else ""
        out["on"] = _bool(raw.get("on"), bool(out["media"]))
        out["angle"] = _flt(raw.get("angle"), -35.0, 35.0, 12.0)
        sd = raw.get("side")
        sd = sd.strip().lower() if isinstance(sd, str) else ""
        out["side"] = sd if sd in SIDES else "auto"
        out["pos"] = _flt(raw.get("pos"), 0.15, 0.85, None)
        out["labels"] = _labels(raw.get("labels"))
        out["color"] = _hex(raw.get("color"), "#19d3ff")
        out["seam_width"] = _flt(raw.get("seam_width"), 0.5, 20.0, 3.0)
        out["wipe"] = _flt(raw.get("wipe"), 0.1, 3.0, 0.55)
        out["seg_start"], out["seg_end"] = _secs(raw.get("seg_start")), _secs(raw.get("seg_end"))
        trig = raw.get("trigger")
        out["trigger"] = trig.strip() if isinstance(trig, str) else ""
        out["start"] = raw["start"] if raw.get("start") is not None else (None if out["trigger"] else 0.0)
        out["end"], out["hold"] = raw.get("end"), raw.get("hold")
    except Exception:
        d = copy.deepcopy(T4_SPLIT)
        d["labels"] = _labels(None)
        return d
    return out


# ---- geometry ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _clip(poly, P, n, off):
    """Sutherland-Hodgman: keep the part of `poly` where (p - P) . n >= off."""
    out = []
    for i, a in enumerate(poly):
        b = poly[(i + 1) % len(poly)]
        da = (a[0] - P[0]) * n[0] + (a[1] - P[1]) * n[1] - off
        db = (b[0] - P[0]) * n[0] + (b[1] - P[1]) * n[1] - off
        if da >= 0:
            out.append(a)
        if (da >= 0) != (db >= 0):
            t = da / (da - db)
            out.append((a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * t, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t))
    return out


def _geometry(W, H, side, angle, pos):
    """Seam line through P with direction d (+ angle = '/'), n = unit normal pointing to the media side, final media polygon, projections."""
    a = math.radians(angle)
    horiz = side in ("top", "bottom")
    d = (math.cos(a), -math.sin(a)) if horiz else (math.sin(a), -math.cos(a))
    n = (-d[1], d[0])
    want = {"bottom": (0, 1), "top": (0, -1), "right": (1, 0), "left": (-1, 0)}[side]
    if n[0] * want[0] + n[1] * want[1] < 0:
        n = (-n[0], -n[1])
    P = (W / 2.0, H * pos) if horiz else (W * pos, H / 2.0)
    rect = [(0.0, 0.0), (float(W), 0.0), (float(W), float(H)), (0.0, float(H))]
    pn = [(x - P[0]) * n[0] + (y - P[1]) * n[1] for x, y in rect]
    pd = [(x - P[0]) * d[0] + (y - P[1]) * d[1] for x, y in rect]
    return {"P": P, "d": d, "n": n, "rect": rect, "poly": _clip(rect, P, n, 0.0), "tmin": min(pd), "tmax": max(pd), "dtot": max(pn) + 2.0}


def _patch(poly, W, H):
    """Even-sized canvas rect (x, y, w, h) that covers the final media polygon, or None for a degenerate polygon."""
    if len(poly) < 3:
        return None
    x0, y0 = max(0, int(math.floor(min(p[0] for p in poly)))), max(0, int(math.floor(min(p[1] for p in poly))))
    x1, y1 = min(W, int(math.ceil(max(p[0] for p in poly)))), min(H, int(math.ceil(max(p[1] for p in poly))))
    if (x1 - x0) % 2:
        x1, x0 = (x1 + 1, x0) if x1 < W else (x1, x0 - 1) if x0 > 0 else (x1 - 1, x0)
    if (y1 - y0) % 2:
        y1, y0 = (y1 + 1, y0) if y1 < H else (y1, y0 - 1) if y0 > 0 else (y1 - 1, y0)
    return (x0, y0, x1 - x0, y1 - y0) if x1 - x0 >= 2 and y1 - y0 >= 2 else None


def _fps(cfg):
    try:
        v = cfg.get("video")
        if v and os.path.isfile(str(v)):
            f = float(probe(str(v))["fps"])
            if f > 0:
                return f
    except (Exception, SystemExit):
        pass
    return 30.0


def _placeholder():
    return Image.new("RGB", (640, 480), (112, 116, 124))


def _media(c):
    """-> {kind 'video'|'image', path, img (images)}; missing / unreadable -> gray placeholder image + one warning."""
    p = c["media"]
    name = os.path.basename(p) or "(none)"
    if p and os.path.isfile(p):
        if p.lower().endswith(VIDEO_EXTS):
            return {"kind": "video", "path": p}
        try:
            with Image.open(p) as im:
                im.seek(0)
                return {"kind": "image", "path": p, "img": im.convert("RGB")}
        except Exception:
            t4_warn("split: '%s' is not a readable image - gray placeholder." % name)
    else:
        t4_warn("split: media '%s' is missing - gray placeholder." % name)
    return {"kind": "image", "path": p, "img": _placeholder()}


def t4_split_plan(cfg, duration, fps=None):
    """Everything the Items and the baked clip share: window snapped to the frame grid (j0 / j1 frames, t0 / t1 s), wipe time, geometry, patch rect,
    media. None when the module is off / has no canvas / its window is empty. Never raises for user data."""
    t4 = cfg.get("t4") if isinstance(cfg, dict) and isinstance(cfg.get("t4"), dict) else {}
    c = t4_split_cfg(t4.get("split"))
    if not c["on"]:
        return None
    try:
        W, H = int(cfg["canvas"][0]), int(cfg["canvas"][1])
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
        t4_warn("t4 split: the job has no canvas - skipped.")
        return None
    win = _window(cfg, c, "split screen", duration, 6.0)
    if win is None:
        return None
    fps = float(fps) if fps else _fps(cfg)
    j0, j1 = int(round(win[0] * fps)), int(round(win[1] * fps))
    if j1 - j0 < 2:
        return None
    side = c["side"] if c["side"] in SIDES else ("bottom" if H > W else "right")
    g = _geometry(W, H, side, c["angle"], c["pos"] if c["pos"] is not None else POS_DEFAULT[side])
    patch = _patch(g["poly"], W, H)
    if patch is None:
        t4_warn("t4 split: the seam leaves no room for the media - skipped.")
        return None
    wipe = max(2.0 / fps, min(c["wipe"], (j1 - j0) / fps / 2.5))
    return dict(g, c=c, W=W, H=H, s=min(W, H) / 1080.0, fps=fps, j0=j0, j1=j1, t0=j0 / fps, t1=j1 / fps, wipe=wipe, side=side, patch=patch,
                media=_media(c))


def t4_split_u(plan, t):
    """Wipe progress 0 (nothing shown) .. 1 (fully shown) at time t: eased entry over `wipe`, hold, the same path back at the end."""
    w = plan["wipe"]
    p = max(0.0, min(1.0, (t - plan["t0"]) / w, (plan["t1"] - t) / w))
    return 1.0 - (1.0 - p) ** 3


def t4_split_mask(plan, u):
    """'L' mask (patch size, anti-aliased) of the media region at progress u, or None when nothing is visible."""
    x0, y0, w, h = plan["patch"]
    off = 0.0 if u >= 1.0 - 1e-6 else (1.0 - u) * plan["dtot"]
    poly = _clip(plan["rect"], plan["P"], plan["n"], off)
    if len(poly) < 3:
        return None
    k = 2
    m = Image.new("L", (w * k, h * k), 0)
    ImageDraw.Draw(m).polygon([((x - x0) * k, (y - y0) * k) for x, y in poly], fill=255)
    return m.resize((w, h), Image.BOX)


def t4_split_cover(img, size):
    """Picture cover-fitted (cropped, never stretched) to size = (w, h), RGB."""
    return ImageOps.fit(img.convert("RGB"), (int(size[0]), int(size[1])), Image.LANCZOS, centering=(0.5, 0.5))


# ---- seam -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _seam_sprite(plan):
    P, d, s, c = plan["P"], plan["d"], plan["s"], plan["c"]
    a = (P[0] + d[0] * (plan["tmin"] - 60), P[1] + d[1] * (plan["tmin"] - 60))
    b = (P[0] + d[0] * (plan["tmax"] + 60), P[1] + d[1] * (plan["tmax"] + 60))
    sp = connector([a, b], c["color"], False, c["seam_width"] * s, 14 * s)
    sp.info["split"] = "seam"
    return sp


def _seam_items(plan, still):
    """The seam sprite is displaced along the normal while it wipes: one Item per frame (frame-exact, same u as the baked clip), one long Item for the hold."""
    sp = _seam_sprite(plan)
    fps, half, items = plan["fps"], 0.5 / plan["fps"], []

    def mk(a, b, off):
        ox, oy = sp.info["origin"]
        return Item(sp, a, b, -ox + sp.width / 2.0 + plan["n"][0] * off, -oy + sp.height / 2.0 + plan["n"][1] * off, "none", 0.0, out=False)
    if still:
        return [mk(plan["t0"], plan["t1"], 0.0)]
    run = None
    for j in range(plan["j0"], plan["j1"]):
        u = t4_split_u(plan, j / fps)
        if u >= 1.0 - 1e-6:
            run = j if run is None else run
            continue
        if run is not None:
            items.append(mk(run / fps - half, j / fps - half, 0.0))
            run = None
        if u > 1e-6:
            items.append(mk(j / fps - half, j / fps + half, (1.0 - u) * plan["dtot"]))
    if run is not None:
        items.append(mk(run / fps - half, plan["j1"] / fps - half, 0.0))
    return items


# ---- labels -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _label_sprite(text, color, font, s, max_w):
    """HUD tag: dark glass panel (chamfered corners, neon border) = number | divider | text, plus L-shaped corner brackets at two opposite corners."""
    m = _NUMRE.match(text)
    num, rest = (m.group(1), m.group(2)) if m else ("", text.strip())
    if not has_arabic(rest):
        rest = rest.upper()
    if not num and not rest:
        return None
    col, rtl, size = t2_rgb(color), is_rtl_text(rest), 30.0 * s
    for _ in range(14):
        nm = t2_text_mask(num, font, size * 1.2, 0.04) if num else None
        rm = t2_text_mask(rest, font, size, 0.10) if rest else None
        gap, padx = size * 0.8, size * 0.9
        total = 2 * padx + (nm.width if nm else 0) + (rm.width if rm else 0) + (2 * gap + 2 if (nm and rm) else 0)
        if total <= max_w or size <= 12 * s:
            break
        size *= 0.9
    ph, g, pw = int(size * 2.3), int(36 * s), int(total)
    cut = ph * 0.28
    base = Image.new("RGBA", (pw + 2 * g, ph + 2 * g), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    kit_paste(base, glass_panel((pw, ph), 0, TINT, col, (cut, 0, cut, 0), max(2.0, 2 * s), 10 * s), g, g)
    b, arm = 9 * s, 24 * s
    for pts in ([(g - b, g - b + arm), (g - b, g - b), (g - b + arm, g - b)],
                [(g + pw + b, g + ph + b - arm), (g + pw + b, g + ph + b), (g + pw + b - arm, g + ph + b)]):
        kit_paste(base, connector(pts, col, False, 3 * s, 8 * s), 0, 0)
    parts = [("n", nm)] if nm else []
    parts += [("d", None)] if (nm and rm) else []
    parts += [("t", rm)] if rm else []
    x = g + padx
    for kind, mk in (parts[::-1] if rtl else parts):
        if kind == "d":
            x += gap
            kit_paste(base, connector([(x, g + ph * 0.25), (x, g + ph * 0.75)], t2_lighten(col, 0.15), False, 1.8 * s, 5 * s), 0, 0)
            x += 2 + gap
            continue
        t2_paint(base, (mk, (int(round(x)), int(round(g + ph / 2.0 - mk.height / 2.0)))), col if kind == "n" else (255, 255, 255))
        x += mk.width
    base.info["split"] = "label"
    return base


def _label_items(cfg, plan, t4):
    c, W, H, s = plan["c"], plan["W"], plan["H"], plan["s"]
    fm = _frame_mask(W, H, t4_frame_cfg(t4.get("frame")), s)
    font, items = _font(c.get("font")), []
    for i, lab in enumerate(c["labels"]):
        if not lab["text"]:
            continue
        spr = _label_sprite(lab["text"], lab["color"] or c["color"], font, s, W * 0.6)
        pl = _place(spr, lab["anchor"] or LABEL_ANCHORS[plan["side"]][i], W, H, s, 0, 0, fm, "split label %d" % (i + 1)) if spr is not None else None
        if pl is None:
            continue
        st = plan["t0"] if i == 0 else plan["t0"] + plan["wipe"] * 0.8
        items.append(Item(spr, min(st, plan["t1"] - 0.1), plan["t1"], pl[0], pl[1], "fade", 0.35, out=True))
    return items


# ---- items ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _still_region(plan):
    """--still only: the media picture (first frame for a video) cut by the final polygon, as one static Item. A real render bakes it instead."""
    from mt.render import _first_frame
    x0, y0, w, h = plan["patch"]
    m = plan["media"]
    img = _first_frame(m["path"]) if m["kind"] == "video" else m["img"]
    mask = t4_split_mask(plan, 1.0)
    if img is None or mask is None:
        return []
    rgba = t4_split_cover(img, (w, h)).convert("RGBA")
    rgba.putalpha(mask)
    rgba.info["split"] = "region"
    return [Item(rgba, plan["t0"], plan["t1"], x0 + w / 2.0, y0 + h / 2.0, "none", 0.0, out=False)]


def t4_split_items(cfg, duration):
    """Items of the split module: [region (--still only)] + seam + labels. [] when off. Never raises."""
    try:
        plan = t4_split_plan(cfg, duration)
        if plan is None:
            return []
        t4 = cfg.get("t4") if isinstance(cfg.get("t4"), dict) else {}
        still = bool(cfg.get("_t2_still"))
        return (_still_region(plan) if still else []) + _seam_items(plan, still) + _label_items(cfg, plan, t4)
    except Exception as e:
        t4_warn("t4 split: could not be drawn (%s: %s) - skipped." % (type(e).__name__, e))
        return []


# ---- questions --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_ask_split():
    """Question group 'split' (called by t4_ask_config). -> the dict for cfg['t4']['split'] or {'on': False}."""
    if not yn("Add a diagonal split-screen (a clip / image wipes in on one side, neon seam + two HUD labels - reference R7)?", default=False):
        return {"on": False}
    media = ask("Split media (image or video path)", key="t4_split_media", cast=c_existing_file(IMAGE_EXTS + VIDEO_EXTS))
    l1 = ask("Label 1, the speaker side ('-' = none)", default=DEFAULT_LABELS[0], key="t4_split_l1", cast=lambda v: v.strip())
    l2 = ask("Label 2, the media side ('-' = none)", default=DEFAULT_LABELS[1], key="t4_split_l2", cast=lambda v: v.strip())
    sd = ask_choice("Media side:", ["Auto (bottom on reels, right on YouTube)", "Bottom", "Top", "Right", "Left"], default=1, key="t4_split_side")
    angle = ask("Seam lean in degrees (0 = straight, negative = the other way)", default=12, key="t4_split_angle", cast=c_float)
    color = ask("Seam colour", default="#19D3FF", key="t4_split_color", cast=c_color)
    out = {"on": True, "media": media, "labels": [l1, l2], "side": ("auto", "bottom", "top", "right", "left")[sd - 1], "angle": angle, "color": color}
    out.update(t4_ask_when("Split screen", 6.0, key="t4_split_when"))
    return out


========================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\split_video.py
========================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.split_video - Template 4: the baking half of the 'split' module. The media (video file / image) is cut by the diagonal polygon at every
frame of the entry wipe, the hold and the exit wipe and written as a small alpha clip (qtrle, only the polygon's bounding box). FFmpeg overlays it as a
video layer with area 'box' (= UNDER the frame plate, above the speaker video) at a fixed position (key '_xy').
The window is snapped to the frame grid (plan j0 / j1) and the same t4_split_u is used by the seam Items, so seam and media edge move together.
Video media: decoded straight at the patch size onto a pipe (one frame in memory), muted; without seg_end it loops, otherwise it plays once and
holds its last frame. Image media: one picture, composed once per distinct wipe step.
Public: t4_split_prepare_layers(cfg, info, tmpdir) -> list of video layers (called by cards_video.t4_prepare_card_layers)."""
import math
import os
import subprocess

from PIL import Image

from mt.common import FFMPEG, _read_exact
from mt.templates.t4.split import t4_split_cover, t4_split_mask, t4_split_plan, t4_split_u
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

GRAY = b"\x5c"


def _reader_cmd(plan, nfr, fps_s):
    c, m = plan["c"], plan["media"]
    _x, _y, w, h = plan["patch"]
    ss, se = c["seg_start"] or 0.0, c["seg_end"]
    if se is not None and se - ss < 0.05:
        se = None
    cmd = [FFMPEG, "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error"]
    if se is None:
        cmd += ["-stream_loop", "-1"]
    cmd += ["-ss", "%.3f" % ss, "-i", str(m["path"])]
    if se is not None:
        cmd += ["-t", "%.3f" % (se - ss)]
    return cmd + ["-an", "-vf", f"fps={fps_s},scale={w}:{h}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={w}:{h},format=rgb24",
                  "-frames:v", str(nfr), "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgb24", "pipe:1"]


def _bake(plan, info, dur_eff, tmpdir):
    fps = info["fps"]
    fps_f = float(fps)
    fps_s = f"{fps.numerator}/{fps.denominator}" if fps.denominator != 1 else str(fps.numerator)
    x0, y0, w, h = plan["patch"]
    j0 = plan["j0"]
    nfr = min(plan["j1"], int(math.ceil(dur_eff * fps_f))) - j0
    if nfr < 1:
        return None
    out = os.path.join(tmpdir, "t4split.mov")
    need, blank, media = w * h * 3, bytes(w * h * 4), plan["media"]
    video = media["kind"] == "video"
    still_pic = None if video else t4_split_cover(media["img"], (w, h))
    print("Preparing split-screen clip (%d frames)..." % nfr)
    rd = subprocess.Popen(_reader_cmd(plan, nfr, fps_s), stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL) if video else None
    wr = subprocess.Popen([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgba", "-video_size", f"{w}x{h}",
                           "-framerate", fps_s, "-i", "pipe:0", "-c:v", "qtrle", "-pix_fmt", "argb", out],
                          stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    last, ckey, cdata = None, None, None
    try:
        for i in range(nfr):
            u = t4_split_u(plan, (j0 + i) / fps_f)
            pic = still_pic
            if video:
                raw = _read_exact(rd.stdout, need)
                raw = raw if len(raw) == need else (last if last is not None else GRAY * need)      # the source ended: hold the last frame
                last = raw
                pic = Image.frombytes("RGB", (w, h), raw)
            if u <= 1e-6:
                data = blank
            elif not video and round(u, 4) == ckey:
                data = cdata
            else:
                mask = t4_split_mask(plan, u)
                if mask is None:
                    data = blank
                else:
                    rgba = pic.convert("RGBA")
                    rgba.putalpha(mask)
                    data = rgba.tobytes()
                ckey, cdata = (None if video else round(u, 4)), data
            wr.stdin.write(data)
        wr.stdin.close()
    except (BrokenPipeError, OSError):
        pass
    finally:
        if rd is not None:
            rd.kill()
            rd.wait()
    err = wr.stderr.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
    if wr.wait() != 0 or not os.path.isfile(out):
        raise RuntimeError(err[-300:] or "FFmpeg could not write the clip")
    return {"path": out, "start": plan["t0"], "end": min(plan["t1"], dur_eff), "area": "box", "mode": "fit", "fade": 0.0, "_xy": [x0, y0]}


def t4_split_prepare_layers(cfg, info, tmpdir):
    """-> [video layer] for the split media, or []. Never raises: a failing bake warns once and the split shows only its seam + labels."""
    try:
        plan = t4_split_plan(cfg, info["duration"], float(info["fps"]))
        if plan is None:
            return []
        dur_eff = info["duration"]
        if cfg.get("preview"):
            dur_eff = min(dur_eff, float(cfg["preview"]))
        if plan["t0"] >= dur_eff:
            return []
        layer = _bake(plan, info, dur_eff, tmpdir)
        return [layer] if layer else []
    except (Exception, SystemExit) as e:
        t4_warn("split: the clip could not be prepared (%s: %s) - skipped." % (type(e).__name__, e))
        return []


========================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\timing.py
========================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.timing - Template 4: when does an overlay module show? Pure functions, no drawing.
t4_time(entry, cues, duration, default_hold) -> (start, end) | None;  t4_stagger(n, step) -> [delay];  t4_ask_when(prompt, default_hold).
t4_warn / t4_warn_reset live in t4/warn.py (P2, once per render - the same pattern as t3_warn) and are re-exported here so every
timing user imports from one place; a second message set would print the same warning twice."""
import math

from mt.captions import find_trigger
from mt.common import ask, ask_choice, c_float, c_time
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn, t4_warn_reset  # noqa: F401  (re-export)

MIN_SPAN = 0.05                                  # a window shorter than this is not worth drawing


def _num(v):
    """Seconds from a number or '12', '0:45', '3s' -> float; anything else (None, bool, nan, junk) -> None. Never raises."""
    if v is None or isinstance(v, bool):
        return None
    try:
        x = float(c_time(v)) if isinstance(v, str) else float(v)
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError):
        return None
    return x if math.isfinite(x) else None


def _streams(cues):
    """cues = one SRT cue list [(s, e, text)] or a list of such lists (every caption stream) -> list of cue lists."""
    try:
        if cues and isinstance(cues[0], (list, tuple)) and cues[0] and isinstance(cues[0][0], (list, tuple)):
            return [c for c in cues if c]
        return [cues] if cues else []
    except (TypeError, IndexError, KeyError):
        return []


def _find(cues, phrase):
    """First stream that contains the phrase wins -> its start time, else None. Never raises."""
    for st in _streams(cues):
        try:
            t = find_trigger(st, phrase)
        except Exception:
            t = None
        if t is not None:
            return float(t)
    return None


def _label(entry):
    for k in ("label", "name", "title", "text", "trigger"):
        v = entry.get(k)
        if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip():
            return "'%s'" % v.strip()[:40]
    return "entry"


def _start(entry, cues, lab):
    """trigger (spoken phrase) first, else the fixed start; None + one warning when neither gives a time."""
    fixed = _num(entry.get("start"))
    phrase = entry.get("trigger")
    phrase = phrase.strip() if isinstance(phrase, str) else ""
    if phrase:
        t = _find(cues, phrase)
        if t is not None:
            return t
        if fixed is None:
            t4_warn("%s: phrase '%s' not found in the captions and no start time - skipped." % (lab, phrase))
            return None
        t4_warn("%s: phrase '%s' not found in the captions - using its start time." % (lab, phrase))
    if fixed is None:
        t4_warn("%s: no start time and no trigger phrase - skipped." % lab)
    return fixed


def t4_time(entry, cues, duration, default_hold):
    """-> (start, end) in seconds, or None (skip the entry).
    start: `trigger` (spoken phrase found in cues via find_trigger; cues = one cue list or a list of streams) else `start`.
    end:   `end` (absolute, must be after start) else start + `hold` else start + default_hold (None / <= 0 -> to the video end).
    Start at or after the video end -> None; an end past the video end is clamped to `duration` (duration None / <= 0 = unknown,
    no clamping). A negative start becomes 0. Junk input never raises (one t4_warn line, None)."""
    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
        t4_warn("timing: entry is not a dict - skipped.")
        return None
    lab = _label(entry)
    st = _start(entry, cues, lab)
    if st is None:
        return None
    st = max(0.0, st)
    dur = _num(duration)
    dur = dur if dur and dur > 0 else None
    if dur is not None and st >= dur:
        t4_warn("%s: starts at %.2f s, after the video end (%.2f s) - skipped." % (lab, st, dur))
        return None
    en = _num(entry.get("end"))
    if en is not None and en <= st + MIN_SPAN:
        t4_warn("%s: end %.2f s is not after the start %.2f s - using the hold time." % (lab, en, st))
        en = None
    if en is None:
        hold = _num(entry.get("hold"))
        if hold is None or hold <= 0:
            hold = _num(default_hold)
        en = st + hold if hold and hold > 0 else (dur if dur is not None else None)
    if en is None:
        t4_warn("%s: no end / hold and the video length is unknown - skipped." % lab)
        return None
    if dur is not None:
        en = min(en, dur)
    return (st, en) if en - st >= MIN_SPAN else None


def t4_stagger(n, step):
    """Entry delays for n items that appear one after another: [0, step, 2*step, ...]. n <= 0 -> []; bad step -> 0.0."""
    try:
        n = max(0, int(n))
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError):
        return []
    s = _num(step)
    s = s if s is not None and s > 0 else 0.0
    return [round(i * s, 6) for i in range(n)]


def _c_hold(s):
    v = c_float(s)
    if not v > 0:
        raise ValueError("enter a number of seconds above 0")
    return v


def t4_ask_when(prompt, default_hold=4.0, key=None):
    """The one shared 'when does it show' question. -> {"start": seconds, "hold": s} or {"trigger": phrase, "hold": s}
    (both shapes are accepted by t4_time). key = optional prefix that remembers the answers (mt.common.D)."""
    k = (lambda n: key + n) if key else (lambda n: None)
    mode = ask_choice(f"{prompt} - when should it appear?",
                      ["At a fixed time (seconds or mm:ss)", "When a phrase is spoken (it must be in the captions)"],
                      default=1, key=k("_mode"))
    if mode == 1:
        out = {"start": ask("Start at  (seconds or mm:ss)", default=0, key=k("_start"), cast=c_time)}
    else:
        out = {"trigger": ask("Spoken phrase", key=k("_trigger")).strip()}
    out["hold"] = ask("Keep it on screen for how many seconds", default=default_hold, key=k("_hold"), cast=_c_hold)
    return out



========================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\frame.py
========================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.frame - Template 4 module 'frame' (ref R10, the frame only): a full-canvas neon HUD frame drawn as ONE transparent
foreground plate (the video underneath is never touched). Parts: thin rails, thick double-line corner brackets (TL / TR / BL / BR),
side tick marks, a small neon icon top-right, and a bottom tab (dark glass trapezoid on the bottom rail) with centred TRACKED text
and '////' hazard stripes at both ends of it.
Config cfg['t4']['frame'] = {on, text, color, icon, show_ticks} (+ optional 'font' = a font file; default = the project's default font).
  text   '' (or '-') = no tab and no stripes (rails + brackets only); Arabic / RTL text is drawn untracked (tracking would break joining).
  icon   a kit icon name (code laptop phone gear globe database server cloud bulb chip shield) or '' / 'none' = no icon.
Public: T4_FRAME, t4_frame_cfg(raw), t4_ask_frame(), t4_frame_plate(W, H, fcfg) -> RGBA W x H (cached), t4_frame_part(W, H, t4) -> plate | None
(the part builder registered in t4/__init__.T4_FG_PARTS). Authored in px at short side 1080 (scaled for other sizes); rails sit 30 px
from the edge (decoration), the tab text stays well inside the 48 px safe margin."""
import os

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

from mt.common import ask, ask_choice, c_color, yn
from mt.draw import t2_glow, t2_lighten, t2_paint, t2_poly_mask, t2_rgb
from mt.fonts import default_font, is_rtl_text, load_font, shape
from mt.templates.t4.cfg import _hex
from mt.templates.t4.kit import _glass_sprite, connector, hazard_stripes, kit_paste, neon_icon
from mt.templates.t4.kit_icons import ICON_NAMES
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

T4_FRAME = {"on": False, "text": "CODE • BUILD • INSPIRE", "color": "#19D3FF", "icon": "code", "show_ticks": True}
INSET = 30                                                    # rail distance from the canvas edge (px at short side 1080)
ARMS = {"tl": (78, 100), "tr": (90, 140), "bl": (70, 84), "br": (70, 84)}      # bracket arms (horizontal, vertical) as in R10
TICKS = {"l": ((0.285, 0.385), (0.64, 0.665)), "r": ((0.27, 0.43), (0.60, 0.64))}   # tick groups: y ranges as fractions of H
_OFF = ("", "none", "off", "false", "no", "0", "-")
_CACHE = {}


# ---- config -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _bool(v, default):
    if isinstance(v, str):
        return default if not v.strip() else v.strip().lower() not in _OFF
    return default if v is None else bool(v)


def t4_frame_cfg(raw):
    """Normalise cfg['t4']['frame'] -> {on, text, color, icon, show_ticks, ...extra keys}. Never raises; junk -> defaults."""
    out = dict(T4_FRAME)
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return out
    try:
        out.update({k: v for k, v in raw.items() if k not in out})            # extras (font ...) survive
        out["on"] = _bool(raw.get("on"), False)
        t = raw.get("text")
        if isinstance(t, str):
            out["text"] = "" if t.strip().lower() in ("-", "none") else t.strip()[:60]
        out["color"] = _hex(raw.get("color"), T4_FRAME["color"])
        ic = raw.get("icon")
        out["icon"] = "" if (ic is False or (isinstance(ic, str) and ic.strip().lower() in _OFF)) \
            else (ic.strip().lower() if isinstance(ic, str) else T4_FRAME["icon"])
        out["show_ticks"] = _bool(raw.get("show_ticks"), True)
    except Exception:
        return dict(T4_FRAME)
    return out


def t4_ask_frame():
    """Question group 'frame' (called by t4_ask_config). -> the frame dict for cfg['t4']['frame']."""
    if not yn("Add the neon HUD frame (corner brackets, side ticks, bottom bar)?", default=False):
        return {"on": False}
    text = ask("Bottom bar text ('-' = no bar; Arabic works too)", default=T4_FRAME["text"], key="t4_frame_text",
               cast=lambda s: "" if s.strip() in ("-", "none") else s.strip())
    color = ask("Frame colour (hex or name)", default=T4_FRAME["color"], key="t4_frame_color", cast=c_color)
