    ic = ask_choice("Small icon in the top-right corner:", ["None"] + list(ICON_NAMES), default=2, key="t4_frame_icon")
    return {"on": True, "text": text, "color": color, "icon": "" if ic == 1 else ICON_NAMES[ic - 2],
            "show_ticks": yn("Side tick marks?", default=True)}


# ---- rails, brackets, ticks, icon -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _bracket(pl, cx, cy, sx, sy, arms, col, s):
    """One corner: thick chamfered L (outer) + a shorter thin line inside it. (cx, cy) = the corner, (sx, sy) = inward direction."""
    ah, av, c, d = arms[0] * s, arms[1] * s, 20 * s, 12 * s
    c2 = max(4.0, c * 0.65)

    def P(pts):
        return [(cx + sx * x, cy + sy * y) for x, y in pts]
    kit_paste(pl, connector(P([(ah, 0), (c, 0), (0, c), (0, av)]), col, False, 9.0 * s, 15 * s), 0, 0)
    kit_paste(pl, connector(P([(d + ah * 0.55, d), (d + c2, d), (d, d + c2), (d, d + av * 0.55)]), col, False, 3.0 * s, 7 * s), 0, 0)


def _rails(pl, W, H, col, s):
    m = INSET * s
    a = {k: (v[0] * s, v[1] * s) for k, v in ARMS.items()}
    for pts in ([(m + a["tl"][0], m), (W - m - a["tr"][0], m)], [(m + a["bl"][0], H - m), (W - m - a["br"][0], H - m)],
                [(m, m + a["tl"][1]), (m, H - m - a["bl"][1])], [(W - m, m + a["tr"][1]), (W - m, H - m - a["br"][1])]):
        kit_paste(pl, connector(pts, col, False, 2.8 * s, 9 * s), 0, 0)
    for key, cx, cy, sx, sy in (("tl", m, m, 1, 1), ("tr", W - m, m, -1, 1), ("bl", m, H - m, 1, -1), ("br", W - m, H - m, -1, -1)):
        _bracket(pl, cx, cy, sx, sy, ARMS[key], col, s)


def _tick_group(pl, x_edge, y0, y1, right, col, s):
    """A column of short horizontal dashes (alternating long / short, fading at both ends) hugging a side rail."""
    pad, step, th = int(10 * s), 12.0 * s, max(2, int(round(4 * s)))
    n = max(1, int((y1 - y0) // step))
    w, h = int(24 * s) + 2 * pad, int(y1 - y0) + 2 * pad
    m = Image.new("L", (w, h), 0)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(m)
    for i in range(n):
        ln = int((21 if i % 2 == 0 else 11) * s)
        fade = min(1.0, 0.35 + 1.3 * min(i, n - 1 - i) / max(1.0, n / 2.0))
        y = pad + int(i * step)
        x0 = (w - pad - ln) if right else pad
        d.rectangle((x0, y, x0 + ln, y + th), fill=int(255 * fade))
    sp = Image.new("RGBA", (w, h), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    c = t2_rgb(col)
    t2_paint(sp, t2_glow((m, (0, 0)), 4 * s, 1.1), c, 0.55)
    t2_paint(sp, (m, (0, 0)), c)
    t2_paint(sp, (m, (0, 0)), t2_lighten(c, 0.6), 0.35)
    kit_paste(pl, sp, (x_edge - (w - pad) if right else x_edge - pad), y0 - pad)


def _ticks(pl, W, H, col, s):
    m = INSET * s
    for side, groups in TICKS.items():
        for a, b in groups:
            _tick_group(pl, (W - m - 14 * s) if side == "r" else (m + 14 * s), H * a, H * b, side == "r", col, s)


def _corner_icon(pl, W, name, col, s):
    sz = int(84 * s)
    ic = neon_icon(name, sz, col)
    kit_paste(pl, ic, W - (INSET + 100) * s - sz / 2.0, (INSET + 98) * s - sz / 2.0)


# ---- bottom bar -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _fit_text(text, fp, size, track, avail):
    """Shrink size, then tracking, then truncate until the run fits `avail` px -> (font, text, track_px, width). RTL: untracked."""
    rtl = is_rtl_text(text)
    for _ in range(60):
        f = load_font(fp, max(8, int(round(size))))
        t = shape(text) if rtl else text
        tp = 0.0 if rtl else track * size
        w = f.getlength(t) if rtl else sum(f.getlength(ch) for ch in t) + tp * max(0, len(t) - 1)
        if w <= avail or len(text) <= 1:
            return f, t, tp, w
        if size > 14:
            size -= 1.5
        elif track > 0.04 and not rtl:
            track -= 0.05
        else:
            text = text[:-2].rstrip() + "…"
    return f, t, tp, w


def _draw_tab_text(pl, f, t, tp, w, cx, base_y, rtl, col, s):
    """White text (tracked per character for LTR) with a faint neon halo."""
    m = Image.new("L", pl.size, 0)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(m)
    if rtl:
        d.text((cx - w / 2.0, base_y), t, font=f, fill=255, anchor="ls")
    else:
        x = cx - w / 2.0
        for ch in t:
            d.text((x, base_y), ch, font=f, fill=255, anchor="ls")
            x += f.getlength(ch) + tp
    bb = m.getbbox()
    if not bb:
        return
    pad = int(14 * s)
    crop = m.crop((bb[0] - pad, bb[1] - pad, bb[2] + pad, bb[3] + pad))
    box = (bb[0] - pad, bb[1] - pad)
    t2_paint(pl, t2_glow((crop, box), 5 * s, 1.0), t2_rgb(col), 0.45)
    t2_paint(pl, (crop, box), (255, 255, 255))


def _hazards(pl, xl, xr, y, hw, hh, col):
    """'////' marks: the group ending at xl (left of the tab) and the one starting at xr (right of it)."""
    for x in (xl - hw, xr):
        kit_paste(pl, hazard_stripes((hw, hh), 32.0, col, 8, hh * 0.52, hh * 0.40), x, y)


def _bottom_bar(pl, W, H, text, fp, col, s):
    m, th, sl = INSET * s, 60 * s, 34 * s
    rtl = is_rtl_text(text)
    bw_max = min(0.64 * W, 860 * s)
    f, t, tp, tw = _fit_text(text, fp, 26 * s, 0.30, bw_max - 2 * sl - 80 * s)
    top_w = tw + 80 * s
    bw = top_w + 2 * sl
    x0, yb = (W - bw) / 2.0, H - m
    yt = yb - th
    mask = t2_poly_mask([(sl, 0), (bw - sl, 0), (bw, th), (0, th)], round_r=5 * s)
    kit_paste(pl, _glass_sprite(mask, (6, 14, 34, 218), col, max(2.0, 2.4 * s), 12 * s), x0, yt)
    asc, desc = f.getmetrics()
    _draw_tab_text(pl, f, t, tp, tw, W / 2.0, (yt + yb) / 2.0 + (asc - desc) / 2.0, rtl, col, s)
    hw = min(108 * s, x0 - 40 * s - (m + 14 * s))
    if hw >= 40 * s:
        _hazards(pl, x0 - 40 * s, x0 + bw + 40 * s, yb - 44 * s, hw, 26 * s, col)


# ---- plate ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_frame_plate(W, H, fcfg):
    """The transparent W x H RGBA frame plate for a normalised frame dict (cached per size + look; returns a private copy)."""
    W, H = int(W), int(H)
    fp = fcfg.get("font")
    fp = fp if isinstance(fp, str) and os.path.isfile(fp) else default_font()
    ic = fcfg["icon"] if fcfg["icon"] in ICON_NAMES else ""
    key = (W, H, fcfg["color"], fcfg["text"], ic, bool(fcfg["show_ticks"]), fp)
    if key not in _CACHE:
        s, col = min(W, H) / 1080.0, fcfg["color"]
        pl = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        _rails(pl, W, H, col, s)
        if fcfg["show_ticks"]:
            _ticks(pl, W, H, col, s)
        if ic:
            _corner_icon(pl, W, ic, col, s)
        if fcfg["text"]:
            _bottom_bar(pl, W, H, fcfg["text"], fp, col, s)
        if len(_CACHE) >= 4:
            _CACHE.pop(next(iter(_CACHE)))
        _CACHE[key] = pl
    return _CACHE[key].copy()


def t4_frame_part(W, H, t4):
    """Part builder for t4_compose_fg: the frame plate, or None when the module is off. Warns once for an unknown icon."""
    f = t4_frame_cfg((t4 or {}).get("frame"))
    if not f["on"]:
        return None
    if f["icon"] and f["icon"] not in ICON_NAMES:
        t4_warn(f"frame icon '{f['icon']}' unknown (use one of: {', '.join(ICON_NAMES)}) - no icon")
    return t4_frame_plate(W, H, f)



========================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\base.py
========================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.base - Template 4: the BASE video's backdrop (what shows around a 'fit' video): solid colour, image, or a blurred
copy of the video itself. Fill mode needs no backdrop (the video covers the canvas), so image / blur are only built for fit."""
import os

from PIL import Image, ImageFilter

from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

BLUR_DIV = 8                       # the blur is made at 1/8 size (fast, smooth) and scaled back up
_BLACK = {"type": "color", "value": "#000000"}


def t4_blur_params(strength, W, H):
    """strength 1..10 -> {W, H, sw, sh, lr}: lr = box-blur radius at the 1/8 size (clamped so ffmpeg's chroma limit is never hit)."""
    sw, sh = max(4, int(round(W / float(BLUR_DIV)))), max(4, int(round(H / float(BLUR_DIV))))
    try:
        s = max(1, min(10, int(strength)))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        s = 5
    return {"W": int(W), "H": int(H), "sw": sw, "sh": sh, "lr": max(1, min(int(round(s * 1.2)), min(sw, sh) // 4 - 1))}


def t4_blur_filter(p):
    """ffmpeg filter chain turning the source copy [rb] into the blurred backdrop: cover-scale+crop at 1/8 size, boxblur, scale up."""
    return (f"scale={p['sw']}:{p['sh']}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={p['sw']}:{p['sh']},"
            f"boxblur={p['lr']}:2,scale={p['W']}:{p['H']}:flags=bicubic,setsar=1,format=yuv420p")


def t4_blur_still(frame, W, H, p):
    """PIL twin of t4_blur_filter for --still: blurred cover copy of `frame` (RGB) at W x H, RGBA."""
    s = max(W / float(frame.width), H / float(frame.height))
    fr = frame.convert("RGB").resize((max(W, int(round(frame.width * s))), max(H, int(round(frame.height * s)))), Image.BILINEAR)
    l, t = (fr.width - W) // 2, (fr.height - H) // 2
    small = fr.crop((l, t, l + W, t + H)).resize((p["sw"], p["sh"]), Image.LANCZOS)
    small = small.filter(ImageFilter.BoxBlur(p["lr"])).filter(ImageFilter.BoxBlur(p["lr"]))
    return small.resize((W, H), Image.BICUBIC).convert("RGBA")


def _image_ok(path):
    try:
        if path and os.path.isfile(str(path)):
            with Image.open(str(path)) as im:
                im.verify()
            return True
    except Exception:
        pass
    return False


def t4_apply_backdrop(cfg, t4):
    """Set cfg['background'] (+ cfg['_t4_blur'] for the blur type) from the normalised t4 dict. Never raises: a missing / broken
    image falls back to the solid colour with one t4_warn line."""
    bd, W, H = t4["backdrop"], cfg["canvas"][0], cfg["canvas"][1]
    cfg.pop("_t4_blur", None)
    cfg["background"] = {"type": "color", "value": bd["value"]}
    if t4["fit"] != "fit":
        return
    if bd["type"] == "image":
        if _image_ok(bd["path"]):
            cfg["background"] = {"type": "image", "path": str(bd["path"])}
        else:
            t4_warn(f"backdrop image '{os.path.basename(str(bd['path'])) or '(none)'}' not found or unreadable - using the solid colour")
    elif bd["type"] == "blur":
        cfg["_t4_blur"] = t4_blur_params(bd["strength"], W, H)



========================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\__init__.py
========================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4 - Template 4 "Overlay Studio" (registry id 5): hooks. Config namespace cfg["t4"] (see cfg.py).
T4-0: only the plain video on a solid backdrop; the design modules (frame, nameplate, tiles, ...) arrive in later tasks."""
import os
import tempfile

from mt.templates.t4.captions import t4_caption_items, t4_caption_style  # noqa: F401  (t4_caption_style = registry hook)
from mt.common import D, IMAGE_EXTS, ask, ask_choice, c_color, c_existing_file, c_int, probe
from mt.templates.t4.base import t4_apply_backdrop
from mt.templates.t4.cards import t4_ask_image_cards, t4_cards_cfg, t4_cards_items
from mt.templates.t4.cards_video import t4_prepare_card_layers  # noqa: F401  (registry hook prepare_layers, id 5)
from mt.templates.t4.frame import t4_ask_frame, t4_frame_cfg, t4_frame_part
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import t4_ask_corner_list, t4_ask_nameplate, t4_corner_list_cfg, t4_nameplate_cfg, t4_nameplate_items
from mt.templates.t4.tiles import t4_ask_tiles, t4_tiles_cfg, t4_tiles_items
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn, t4_warn_reset
from mt.templates.t4.scenes import t4_ask_scenes, t4_scenes_cfg, t4_scenes_items
from mt.templates.t4.split import t4_ask_split, t4_split_cfg, t4_split_items
from mt.templates.t4.cfg import CANVAS_REELS, T4_DEFAULTS, T4_MODULES, t4_canvas, t4_cfg  # noqa: F401


def _full_box(canvas, fit):
    return {"x": 0, "y": 0, "w": int(canvas[0]), "h": int(canvas[1]), "mode": fit}


def t4_ask_config(video_info):
    """Canvas (auto / reels / youtube), fill or fit, letterbox colour (fit only). -> tpl dict; video_box = the whole canvas."""
    print("\n=== Template 4 - Overlay Studio ===")
    cm = ask_choice("Canvas:", ["Auto (by the video shape)", "Reels / Shorts 1080x1920", "YouTube 1920x1080"], default=1,
                    key="t4_canvas")
    mode = ("auto", "reels", "youtube")[cm - 1]
    canvas = t4_canvas(mode, video_info)
    print(f"   -> canvas {canvas[0]}x{canvas[1]}")
    ft = ask_choice("Video inside the canvas:", ["Fill (crop to the canvas)", "Fit (whole video, letterboxed)"], default=1,
                    key="t4_fit")
    fit = "fill" if ft == 1 else "fit"
    t4 = {"canvas_mode": mode, "fit": fit}
    if fit == "fit":
        t4["backdrop"] = _ask_backdrop()
    t4["frame"] = t4_ask_frame()                       # question group 'frame' (T4-8)
    t4["nameplate"] = t4_ask_nameplate()               # question groups 'nameplate' (tag_a / tag_b) and 'corner_list' (T4-9)
    t4["corner_list"] = t4_ask_corner_list()
    t4["tiles"] = t4_ask_tiles()                       # question group 'tiles' (T4-10): a list with one group, or {'on': False}
    t4["cards"] = t4_ask_image_cards()                 # question group 'cards' (T4-11): a list with one image card, or {'on': False}
    t4["scenes"] = t4_ask_scenes()
    t4["split"] = t4_ask_split()
    t4 = t4_cfg(t4)
    return {"template_id": 5, "canvas": canvas, "video_box": _full_box(canvas, fit), "background": {"type": "color", "value": t4["backdrop"]["value"]},
            "layers": [], "t4": t4}


def _ask_backdrop():
    """What shows around a 'fit' video: solid colour / image / blurred copy of the video. -> backdrop dict (t4_cfg normalises it)."""
    kind = ask_choice("Around the video (backdrop):", ["Solid colour (black by default)", "Image", "Blurred copy of the video"],
                      default=1, key="t4_backdrop_type")
    if kind == 2:
        return {"type": "image", "path": ask("Backdrop image (PNG/JPG...)", cast=c_existing_file(IMAGE_EXTS))}
    if kind == 3:
        return {"type": "blur", "strength": ask("Blur strength 1-10", default=5, key="t4_blur", cast=c_int)}
    return {"type": "color", "value": ask("Backdrop colour (hex or name)", default="#000000", key="t4_backdrop", cast=c_color)}


def t4_ask_cards(cues):
    return []                      # Template 4 has no generic cards; its card modules come later (cfg["t4"]["cards"])


def _canvas_ok(c):
    try:
        return len(c) == 2 and all(int(v) > 0 for v in c)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return False


# ---- the T4 foreground plate (cfg['_t2_fg']): ONE transparent full-canvas PNG over the video, merged from the static parts ---------------
# Each part = (name, builder); builder(W, H, t4) -> RGBA image W x H | None (module off). List order = bottom -> top. A later module
# (nameplate, tags, ...) adds ONE line here: T4_FG_PARTS.append(("nameplate", t4_nameplate_part)) - never its own _t2_fg.
T4_FG_PARTS = [("frame", t4_frame_part)]


def t4_compose_fg(cfg, tmp):
    """Alpha-composite every active part into one plate, save tmp/t4_fg.png and point cfg['_t2_fg'] at it (the pipeline's generic
    foreground slot). No active part -> cfg['_t2_fg'] removed, returns None. A failing part warns once and is skipped; never raises."""
    cfg.pop("_t2_fg", None)
    try:
        W, H = int(cfg["canvas"][0]), int(cfg["canvas"][1])
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
        return None
    plate = None
    for name, build in T4_FG_PARTS:
        try:
            part = build(W, H, cfg.get("t4"))
        except Exception as e:
            t4_warn(f"{name}: could not be drawn ({type(e).__name__}: {e}) - skipped")
            continue
        if part is None:
            continue
        if part.size != (W, H):
            t4_warn(f"{name}: plate is {part.size[0]}x{part.size[1]}, canvas is {W}x{H} - skipped")
            continue
        if plate is None:
            plate = part.convert("RGBA")
        else:
            plate.alpha_composite(part.convert("RGBA"))
    if plate is None:
        return None
    path = os.path.join(tmp or tempfile.gettempdir(), "t4_fg.png")
    plate.save(path, compress_level=1)
    cfg["_t2_fg"] = path
    return path


def t4_build_static_layers(cfg, tmp):
    """Hook build_background: solid backdrop + the foreground plate. The video is the base, so video_box is forced to the full canvas
    (fill / fit from cfg["t4"]); a job without a usable canvas gets one from canvas_mode + the video shape."""
    t4_warn_reset()
    t4 = t4_cfg(cfg.get("t4"))
    cfg["t4"] = t4
    if not _canvas_ok(cfg.get("canvas")):
        info = None
        try:
            info = probe(cfg["video"]) if cfg.get("video") and os.path.isfile(str(cfg["video"])) else None
        except Exception:
            info = None
        cfg["canvas"] = t4_canvas(t4["canvas_mode"], info)
    cfg["canvas"] = [int(cfg["canvas"][0]), int(cfg["canvas"][1])]
    cfg["video_box"] = _full_box(cfg["canvas"], t4["fit"])
    t4_apply_backdrop(cfg, t4)
    t4["frame"] = t4_frame_cfg(t4.get("frame"))        # complete {on, text, color, icon, show_ticks} (extras kept)
    t4["nameplate"] = t4_nameplate_cfg(t4.get("nameplate"))          # complete dicts (extras kept); drawn as Items by t4_build_items
    t4["corner_list"] = t4_corner_list_cfg(t4.get("corner_list"))
    t4["tiles"] = t4_tiles_cfg(t4.get("tiles")) or {"on": False}      # complete groups (extras kept), drawn as Items by t4_build_items
    t4["cards"] = t4_cards_cfg(t4.get("cards")) or {"on": False}      # complete image cards (extras kept), drawn as Items by t4_build_items
    t4["scenes"] = t4_scenes_cfg(t4.get("scenes")) or {"on": False}
    t4["split"] = t4_split_cfg(t4.get("split"))                       # complete dict; drawn by t4_build_items (seam + labels) + baked clip
    t4_compose_fg(cfg, tmp)


def t4_build_items(cfg, duration, splits, lbox, rbox):
    return t4_split_items(cfg, duration) + t4_caption_items(cfg, duration) + t4_nameplate_items(cfg, duration) + t4_tiles_items(cfg, duration) + t4_cards_items(cfg, duration) + t4_scenes_items(cfg, duration)

"""Split (R7) check: python tools/mt_t4_split_check.py   (also writes out/t4/split_view_{reels,youtube}.png + split_view_ar_reels.png - LOOK at them)."""
import contextlib, io, json, math, os, shutil, subprocess, sys, tempfile
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageChops
P = Path(os.environ.get("MT_PROJECT", Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))
sys.path.insert(0, str(P)); sys.argv = ["montage.py", "--help"]
import mt.templates                                                                       # noqa: E402
from mt.common import FFMPEG, probe                                                       # noqa: E402
from mt.render import render_still                                                        # noqa: E402
from mt.templates.t4.cfg import t4_cfg                                                    # noqa: E402
from mt.templates.t4.frame import t4_frame_cfg, t4_frame_plate                            # noqa: E402
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import FRAME_THR, INK_THR                                  # noqa: E402
from mt.templates.t4.split import t4_split_cfg, t4_split_items, t4_split_plan, t4_split_u  # noqa: E402
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn_reset                                            # noqa: E402
rows = []
def rec(name, ok, detail=""):
    rows.append(bool(ok)); print(f"{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL':5} {name}  {detail}")
def quiet(fn, *a):
    buf = io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buf): r = fn(*a)
    return r, [l for l in buf.getvalue().splitlines() if "[!]" in l]
CV = {"reels": (1080, 1920), "youtube": (1920, 1080)}
TAN = math.tan(math.radians(12))
tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="t4split_")); OUT = P / "out" / "t4"; OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
def ff(*a): subprocess.run([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", *a], check=True)
def mag(a): return (a[..., 0] > 150) & (a[..., 2] > 150) & (a[..., 1] < 100)
def job(cv, split, video, frame=False):
    W, H = CV[cv]
    return {"template_id": 5, "video": str(video), "canvas": [W, H], "video_box": {"x": 0, "y": 0, "w": W, "h": H, "mode": "fill"},
            "background": {"type": "color", "value": "#000000"}, "t4": t4_cfg({"canvas_mode": cv, "fit": "fill", "split": split, "frame": {"on": frame}}),
            "layers": [], "captions": [], "cards": [], "texts": [], "elements": [], "video_layers": []}
def SPL(media, **k): return dict({"on": True, "media": str(media)}, **k)
try:
    SPK, VIEW, DEV, MAG = tmp / "spk.mp4", tmp / "view.mp4", tmp / "dev.mp4", tmp / "mag.png"
    ff("-f", "lavfi", "-i", "color=c=0x20a020:s=360x640:r=25:d=4", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=f=440:d=4", "-c:v", "libx264", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-c:a", "aac", "-shortest", str(SPK))
    ff("-f", "lavfi", "-i", "smptebars=s=640x360:r=25:d=4", "-c:v", "libx264", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", str(VIEW))
    ff("-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=s=640x360:r=25:d=4", "-c:v", "libx264", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", str(DEV))
    Image.new("RGB", (400, 300), (200, 30, 200)).save(MAG)
    FPS = float(probe(str(SPK))["fps"])
    # ---- cfg ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    junk = [None, 5, "x", [], {"angle": "zz", "side": 5, "pos": 9, "labels": "x", "wipe": -1, "media": 7, "color": "zz"}]
    cs = [t4_split_cfg(j) for j in junk]
    ok = all(c["on"] is False and c["angle"] == 12.0 and c["side"] == "auto" and c["pos"] is None and c["wipe"] == 0.55 and len(c["labels"]) == 2 and c["labels"][0]["text"].startswith("01") for c in cs)
    e = t4_split_cfg({"media": "a.png", "labels": ["-", {"text": "B", "anchor": "tl"}], "angle": 99, "side": "RIGHT", "pos": 0.3})
    ok = ok and e["on"] is True and e["labels"][0]["text"] == "" and e["labels"][1] == {"text": "B", "color": "", "anchor": "top-left"} and e["angle"] == 12.0 and e["side"] == "right" and e["pos"] == 0.3
    rec("split cfg: junk -> defaults (never raises), labels '-' hides / anchor alias, side + pos kept, angle out of range -> default, on follows media", ok, f"{cs[-1]} {e}")
    # ---- geometry -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    area = lambda p: abs(sum(a[0] * b[1] - b[0] * a[1] for a, b in zip(p, p[1:] + p[:1]))) / 2.0
    for cv, (W, H) in CV.items():
        plan, w = quiet(t4_split_plan, job(cv, SPL(MAG), SPK), 6.0, 25.0)
        d = plan["d"]; want = 0.5 if cv == "reels" else 0.4
        slant = abs(d[1] / d[0]) if cv == "reels" else abs(d[0] / d[1])
        side_ok = plan["side"] == ("bottom" if cv == "reels" else "right")
        us = [t4_split_u(plan, (plan["j0"] + k) / 25.0) for k in range(int(plan["wipe"] * 25) + 3)]
        mid, tail = t4_split_u(plan, (plan["t0"] + plan["t1"]) / 2), t4_split_u(plan, plan["t1"] - 1 / 25.0)
        rec(f"split geometry {cv}: auto side, media area {want:.0%}, seam lean 12 deg ({'near-horizontal' if cv == 'reels' else 'near-vertical slant on the right'}), wipe u 0 -> 1 -> back",
            side_ok and abs(area(plan["poly"]) / (W * H) - want) < 0.01 and abs(slant - TAN) < 0.01 and us[0] == 0 and all(b >= a for a, b in zip(us, us[1:])) and mid == 1.0 and tail < 0.3 and not w,
            f"side={plan['side']} area={area(plan['poly']) / (W * H):.3f} slant={slant:.3f} u={[round(x, 2) for x in us[:6]]} tail={tail:.2f} warn={w}")
    # ---- items (no render): seam steps, labels in the safe margin, frame clear -------------------------------------------------------
    def ink(it, W, H):
        full = Image.new("L", (W, H), 0)
        full.paste(it.sprite.getchannel("A").point(lambda v: 255 if v > INK_THR else 0), (int(it.cx - it.sprite.width / 2), int(it.cy - it.sprite.height / 2)))
        return full
    bad = []
    for cv, (W, H) in CV.items():
        for fo in (False, True):
            fa = t4_frame_plate(W, H, t4_frame_cfg({"on": True})).getchannel("A").point(lambda v: 255 if v > FRAME_THR else 0) if fo else None
            t4_warn_reset(); items, w = quiet(t4_split_items, job(cv, SPL(MAG, labels=["01 \u2014 TALKING HEAD", "02 \u2014 LIVE DEVELOPMENT"]), SPK, fo), 6.0)
            seam = [i for i in items if i.sprite.info.get("split") == "seam"]; labs = [i for i in items if i.sprite.info.get("split") == "label"]
            steps = [i for i in seam if i.end - i.start < 0.5]; hold = [i for i in seam if i.end - i.start >= 0.5]
            if w or len(labs) != 2 or len(hold) != 1 or len(steps) < 6 or any(i.sprite.info.get("split") == "region" for i in items) \
                    or any(abs((i.end - i.start) - 1 / FPS) > 1e-6 for i in steps):
                bad.append(f"{cv}/{fo}: labels={len(labs)} hold={len(hold)} steps={len(steps)} warn={w}")
            for it in labs:
                bb = ink(it, W, H).getbbox(); s = min(W, H) / 1080.0
                hit = fa is not None and ImageChops.multiply(ink(it, W, H), fa).getbbox() is not None
                if not bb or bb[0] < 47 * s or bb[1] < 47 * s or W - bb[2] < 47 * s or H - bb[3] < 47 * s or hit:
                    bad.append(f"{cv}/{fo}: label ink={bb} frame_hit={hit}")
    rec("split items (both canvases x frame off / on): 1 hold seam + per-frame seam steps (1 frame each), 2 labels >= 48 px from the edges and clear of the frame, no region item in a real render", not bad, "; ".join(bad[:3]))
    st_items, _w = quiet(t4_split_items, dict(job("reels", SPL(MAG), SPK), _t2_still=True), 6.0)
    rec("split items in --still mode: 1 region + 1 seam + 2 labels", sorted(i.sprite.info.get("split") for i in st_items) == ["label", "label", "region", "seam"])
    # ---- stills: pixels --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    for cv, (W, H) in CV.items():
        stp, ofp = tmp / f"s_{cv}.png", tmp / f"o_{cv}.png"
        quiet(render_still, job(cv, SPL(MAG), SPK), str(stp), 2.0); quiet(render_still, job(cv, {"on": False}, SPK), str(ofp), 2.0)
        a, b = np.asarray(Image.open(stp).convert("RGB"), int), np.asarray(Image.open(ofp).convert("RGB"), int)
        M = mag(a)
        pm, ps = ((W // 2, int(H * .9)), (W // 2, int(H * .1))) if cv == "reels" else ((int(W * .92), H // 2), (int(W * .1), H // 2))
        pix_ok = bool(M[pm[1], pm[0]]) and np.abs(a[ps[1], ps[0]] - np.array([32, 160, 32])).max() < 45
        try:
            if cv == "reels":
                dv = np.where(M[:, int(.1 * W)])[0].min() - np.where(M[:, int(.9 * W)])[0].min(); exp = .8 * W * TAN
            else:
                dv = np.where(M[int(.2 * H), :])[0].min() - np.where(M[int(.8 * H), :])[0].min(); exp = .6 * H * TAN
            slant_ok = dv > 0 and abs(dv - exp) < 22
        except ValueError:
            dv, exp, slant_ok = None, None, False
        if cv == "reels": strip = a[H // 2 - 12:H // 2 + 13, W // 2]
        else: strip = a[H // 2, int(.6 * W) - 12:int(.6 * W) + 13]
        seam_ok = bool(((strip[:, 1] > 170) & (strip[:, 2] > 200)).any())
        reg = (slice(int(.15 * H), int(.35 * H)), slice(None)) if cv == "reels" else (slice(int(.2 * H), int(.8 * H)), slice(int(.1 * W), int(.4 * W)))
        same = float(np.abs(a[reg] - b[reg]).mean()) < 2.0
        rec(f"split still {cv}: media colour on its side only, speaker side untouched, seam leans 12 deg the right way, cyan seam pixels on the diagonal",
            pix_ok and slant_ok and seam_ok and same, f"media/speaker px ok={pix_ok} lean={dv} (want ~{exp and round(exp)}) seam={seam_ok} speaker side unchanged={same}")
    for cv in CV:
        quiet(render_still, job(cv, SPL(DEV, hold=5), VIEW, True), str(OUT / f"split_view_{cv}.png"), 2.0)
    quiet(render_still, job("reels", SPL(DEV, hold=5, labels=["\u0660\u0661 \u2014 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062a\u062d\u062f\u062b", "\u0660\u0662 \u2014 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0637\u0648\u064a\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0628\u0627\u0634\u0631"]), VIEW, False), str(OUT / "split_view_ar_reels.png"), 2.0)
    # ---- real renders: wipe in / hold / wipe out ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def grab(mp4, t, name):
        png = tmp / name; ff("-ss", str(t), "-i", str(mp4), "-frames:v", "1", str(png)); return np.asarray(Image.open(png).convert("RGB"), int)
    for cv, (W, H) in CV.items():
        jp, out = tmp / f"r_{cv}.json", tmp / f"r_{cv}.mp4"
        jb = job(cv, SPL(MAG, start=0.5, hold=2.5), SPK); jb["output"] = str(out); jp.write_text(json.dumps(jb), encoding="utf-8")
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(P / "montage.py"), "--job", str(jp), "--arabic-mode", "raqm"], cwd=str(tmp), capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        if r.returncode != 0 or not out.is_file():
            rec(f"split render {cv}", False, (r.stdout + r.stderr)[-300:]); continue
        i = probe(str(out)); full = 0.5 if cv == "reels" else 0.4
        c = {t: float(mag(grab(out, t, f"g_{cv}_{t}.png")).mean()) for t in (0.35, 0.7, 1.6, 2.8, 3.5)}
        rec(f"split render {cv}: size / duration / audio, then nothing -> partial wipe -> full media -> partial (exit) -> nothing",
            (i["w"], i["h"]) == (W, H) and abs(i["duration"] - 4) < 0.25 and bool(i["audio"]) and c[0.35] < 0.01 and 0.1 * full < c[0.7] < 0.97 * full
            and abs(c[1.6] - full) < 0.04 and 0.1 * full < c[2.8] < 0.97 * c[1.6] and c[3.5] < 0.01, f"{i['w']}x{i['h']} {i['duration']:.2f}s coverage={ {k: round(v, 3) for k, v in c.items()} } (full ~{full})")
    jp, out = tmp / "r_clip.json", tmp / "r_clip.mp4"
    jb = job("reels", SPL(DEV, start=0.5, hold=3), SPK); jb["output"] = str(out); jp.write_text(json.dumps(jb), encoding="utf-8")
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(P / "montage.py"), "--job", str(jp), "--arabic-mode", "raqm"], cwd=str(tmp), capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    if out.is_file():
        a1, a2 = grab(out, 1.4, "c1.png"), grab(out, 1.9, "c2.png")
        rec("split render with a VIDEO media: the clip plays (media area differs between 1.4 s and 1.9 s)", float(np.abs(a1[int(.75 * 1920):int(.95 * 1920), 200:800] - a2[int(.75 * 1920):int(.95 * 1920), 200:800]).mean()) > 2.0)
    else:
        rec("split render with a VIDEO media", False, (r.stdout + r.stderr)[-300:])
    # ---- robustness ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    t4_warn_reset(); r1, w1 = quiet(t4_split_items, job("reels", SPL(tmp / "nope.png"), SPK), 6.0)
    t4_warn_reset(); r2, w2 = quiet(t4_split_items, job("reels", SPL(MAG, trigger="never said"), SPK), 6.0)
    t4_warn_reset(); r3, w3 = quiet(t4_split_items, {"t4": t4_cfg({"split": SPL(MAG)})}, 6.0)
    r4 = quiet(t4_split_items, job("reels", {"on": False}, SPK), 6.0)[0]
    rec("split never raises: missing media -> gray placeholder + ONE warning (seam + labels still drawn); trigger not found -> skipped + 1 warning; no canvas -> [] + 1 warning; off -> []",
        len(r1) >= 3 and len(w1) == 1 and "nope.png" in w1[0] and r2 == [] and len(w2) == 1 and r3 == [] and len(w3) == 1 and r4 == [], f"{w1} {w2} {w3}")
finally:
    shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
print(sum(rows), "pass,", len(rows) - sum(rows), "fail"); sys.exit(0 if all(rows) else 1)

FILE: tools\mt_t4_quick.py

"""Template 4 quick check (fast): python tools/mt_t4_quick.py
P1: compile + imports + cfg normaliser + 1.5 s --job renders on BOTH canvases (size / duration / audio).
P3 ('T4-3'): timing.py unit checks (t4_time / t4_stagger / t4_ask_when).
P2 ('T4-2'): base-video modes fill / fit and the backdrops colour / image / blur, measured on the rendered frames."""
import json, os, py_compile, subprocess, sys, tempfile, shutil
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
P = Path(os.environ.get("MT_PROJECT", Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))
sys.path.insert(0, str(P)); sys.argv = ["montage.py", "--help"]
rows = []
def rec(name, ok, detail=""):
    rows.append(bool(ok)); print(f"{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL':5} {name}  {detail}")
# ---- P1 ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
bad = []
for f in list((P / "mt" / "templates" / "t4").glob("*.py")) + [P / "mt" / "templates" / "__init__.py", P / "mt" / "render.py"]:
    try: py_compile.compile(str(f), doraise=True)
    except Exception as e: bad.append(f"{f.name}: {e}")
rec("compile t4 package + templates/__init__ + render", not bad, "; ".join(bad))
import mt.templates                                            # noqa: E402
from mt.common import FFMPEG, TEMPLATE_REGISTRY, probe        # noqa: E402
from mt.templates.t4.cfg import T4_MODULES, t4_canvas, t4_cfg  # noqa: E402
e = TEMPLATE_REGISTRY.get(5, {})
rec("registry id 5", e.get("name") == "Template 4" and all(k in e for k in ("ask_config", "ask_cards", "caption_style", "build_background", "build_items")))
junk = [None, 5, "x", [], {"fit": 3, "canvas_mode": None, "backdrop": "red", "frame": 7, "tiles": {"on": 1, "n": 2}}]
ok = all(set(T4_MODULES) <= set(t4_cfg(j)) and t4_cfg(j)["fit"] == "fill" and t4_cfg(j)["backdrop"]["value"] == "#000000" for j in junk)
ok = ok and t4_cfg(junk[-1])["tiles"] == {"on": True, "n": 2} and t4_cfg({"backdrop": {"value": "#ABC"}})["backdrop"]["value"] == "#aabbcc"
rec("t4_cfg never raises, fills defaults", ok)
ok = (t4_canvas("auto", {"w": 1920, "h": 1080}) == [1920, 1080] and t4_canvas("auto", {"w": 720, "h": 1280}) == [1080, 1920]
      and t4_canvas("auto", None) == [1080, 1920] and t4_canvas("reels", {"w": 9, "h": 1}) == [1080, 1920] and t4_canvas("youtube") == [1920, 1080])
rec("t4_canvas", ok)
# ---- helpers -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="t4q_"))
CV = {"reels": [1080, 1920], "youtube": [1920, 1080]}
def job_for(video, canvas, fit, backdrop=None, **extra):
    t4 = t4_cfg({"canvas_mode": canvas, "fit": fit, "backdrop": backdrop or {}})
    cv = CV[canvas]
    return dict({"template_id": 5, "video": str(video), "canvas": cv, "video_box": {"x": 0, "y": 0, "w": cv[0], "h": cv[1], "mode": fit},
                 "background": {"type": "color", "value": t4["backdrop"]["value"]}, "t4": t4, "layers": [], "captions": [], "cards": [],
                 "texts": [], "elements": [], "video_layers": []}, **extra)
def run(name, job, still=False):
    jp, out = tmp / f"{name}.json", tmp / (f"{name}.png" if still else f"{name}.mp4")
    job = dict(job, output=str(tmp / f"{name}.mp4"), preview=1.5)
    jp.write_text(json.dumps(job), encoding="utf-8")
    args = ["--still", "--job", str(jp), "--still-out", str(out)] if still else ["--job", str(jp)]
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(P / "montage.py"), *args, "--arabic-mode", "raqm"], cwd=str(tmp), capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    return (out if r.returncode == 0 and out.is_file() else None), r.stdout + r.stderr
def frame(path, t="0.7"):
    if str(path).endswith(".png"):
        return np.asarray(Image.open(path).convert("RGB"), float)
    png = tmp / "f.png"
    subprocess.run([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-ss", t, "-i", str(path), "-frames:v", "1", str(png)], check=True)
    return np.asarray(Image.open(png).convert("RGB"), float)
def bbox(a, thr=24):
    """(x0, x1, y0, y1) of the non-black area (pixels brighter than thr on any channel)."""
    m = a.max(axis=2) > thr
    xs, ys = np.where(m.any(axis=0))[0], np.where(m.any(axis=1))[0]
    return (int(xs[0]), int(xs[-1]) + 1, int(ys[0]), int(ys[-1]) + 1) if len(xs) and len(ys) else (0, 0, 0, 0)
def grad(a):
    return float(np.abs(np.diff(a.mean(axis=2), axis=1)).mean())
def mk(name, size, color=None):
    out = tmp / name
    src = f"testsrc2=s={size}:r=25:d=3" if not color else f"color=c={color}:s={size}:r=25:d=3"
    subprocess.run([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", src, "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=f=440:d=3",
                    "-c:v", "libx264", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-c:a", "aac", "-shortest", str(out)], check=True)
    return out
def check_render(name, job, want):
    out, log = run(name, job)
    if not out:
        return rec(f"render {name}", False, log[-300:]), None
    i = probe(str(out)); w, h = want
    rec(f"render {name}", (i["w"], i["h"]) == (w, h) and abs(i["duration"] - 1.5) < 0.2 and bool(i["audio"]), f"{i['w']}x{i['h']} {i['duration']:.2f}s audio={bool(i['audio'])} (want {w}x{h} 1.5s audio)")
    return None, out
try:
    land, port = mk("land.mp4", "640x360"), mk("port.mp4", "360x640")
    # P1 renders (kept)
    for cname in ("reels", "youtube"):
        for fit in ("fill", "fit"):
            check_render(f"{cname}_{fit}", job_for(land, cname, fit), CV[cname])
    check_render("auto_nocanvas", {"template_id": 5, "video": str(land), "t4": {"canvas_mode": "auto"}, "layers": [], "captions": [], "cards": [], "texts": [], "elements": [], "video_layers": []}, (1920, 1080))
    # ---- T4-2: base video modes + backdrops ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # (a) 9:16 clip, 16:9 canvas, fit, black sides (R3): centred full-height column, bars pure black
    out, _ = run("t42_port_yt_fit", job_for(port, "youtube", "fit"))
    a = frame(out) if out else None
    if a is not None:
        x0, x1, y0, y1 = bbox(a); bars = max(a[:, :x0 - 2].max(), a[:, x1 + 2:].max()) if x0 > 2 else 255
        rec("T4-2 9:16 clip on 16:9 canvas, fit: black sides, centred, full height", abs((x0 + x1) / 2 - 960) < 3 and abs((x1 - x0) - 607) < 6 and y0 <= 1 and y1 >= 1079 and bars < 8,
            f"video columns {x0}-{x1} (want ~656-1264), rows {y0}-{y1}, bar max={bars:.0f}")
    else: rec("T4-2 9:16 clip on 16:9 canvas, fit", False, "render failed")
    # (b) 16:9 clip on the 9:16 canvas, fit: full width, centred band
    out, _ = run("t42_land_rl_fit", job_for(land, "reels", "fit"))
    a = frame(out) if out else None
    if a is not None:
        x0, x1, y0, y1 = bbox(a); bars = max(a[:y0 - 2].max(), a[y1 + 2:].max()) if y0 > 2 else 255
        rec("T4-2 16:9 clip on 9:16 canvas, fit: full width, centred band, black bars", x0 <= 1 and x1 >= 1079 and abs((y0 + y1) / 2 - 960) < 3 and abs((y1 - y0) - 607) < 6 and bars < 8,
            f"cols {x0}-{x1}, band rows {y0}-{y1} (want ~656-1264), bar max={bars:.0f}")
    else: rec("T4-2 16:9 clip on 9:16 canvas, fit", False, "render failed")
    # (c) fill covers the canvas on both canvases (no black corner), aspect kept (crop, not stretch)
    for cname, src in (("youtube", port), ("reels", land)):
        out, _ = run(f"t42_fill_{cname}", job_for(src, cname, "fill"))
        a = frame(out) if out else None
        W, H = CV[cname]
        rec(f"T4-2 fill covers the {cname} canvas", a is not None and a.shape[:2] == (H, W) and min(a[:8, :8].max(), a[-8:, -8:].max(), a[:8, -8:].max(), a[-8:, :8].max()) > 24,
            "" if a is not None else "render failed")
    # (d) blur backdrop: sides are NOT black, much smoother than the video, video column untouched
    out, _ = run("t42_port_yt_blur", job_for(port, "youtube", "fit", {"type": "blur", "strength": 5}))
    a = frame(out) if out else None
    if a is not None:
        side, mid = a[:, 20:560], a[:, 700:1220]
        rec("T4-2 blur backdrop (9:16 on 16:9): sides lit + smooth, video sharp", side.mean() > 25 and grad(side) < 0.35 * grad(mid) and bbox(a, 8) == (0, 1920, 0, 1080),
            f"side mean={side.mean():.0f} side grad={grad(side):.2f} video grad={grad(mid):.2f}")
        ref = frame(run("t42_port_yt_fit2", job_for(port, "youtube", "fit"))[0])
        rec("T4-2 blur: the video column itself is identical to the plain fit render", float(np.abs(a[:, 700:1220] - ref[:, 700:1220]).mean()) < 1.5, f"mean diff {float(np.abs(a[:, 700:1220] - ref[:, 700:1220]).mean()):.2f}")
    else: rec("T4-2 blur backdrop", False, "render failed")
    out, log = run("t42_land_rl_blur", job_for(land, "reels", "fit", {"type": "blur", "strength": 8}))
    a = frame(out) if out else None
    rec("T4-2 blur backdrop (16:9 on 9:16): top / bottom bars lit", a is not None and a[:400].mean() > 25 and a[-400:].mean() > 25, "" if a is not None else log[-200:])
    # (e) image backdrop + broken image path
    Image.new("RGB", (64, 64), (200, 30, 30)).save(tmp / "bd.png")
    out, _ = run("t42_img", job_for(port, "youtube", "fit", {"type": "image", "path": str(tmp / "bd.png")}))
    a = frame(out) if out else None
    px = a[540, 100] if a is not None else None
    rec("T4-2 image backdrop (solid red png) fills the side bars", px is not None and abs(px - np.array([200, 30, 30])).max() < 14, f"pixel={None if px is None else px.round().tolist()}")
    out, log = run("t42_img_missing", job_for(port, "youtube", "fit", {"type": "image", "path": str(tmp / "nope.png"), "value": "#202020"}))
    a = frame(out) if out else None
    rec("T4-2 missing backdrop image -> solid colour + one warning, render still ok", a is not None and abs(a[540, 100] - 32).max() < 8 and log.count("backdrop image") == 1, f"warnings={log.count('backdrop image')}")
    # (f) colour backdrop value (16:9 clip on the 9:16 canvas leaves bars above / below)
    out, _ = run("t42_color", job_for(land, "reels", "fit", {"type": "color", "value": "#112233"}))
    a = frame(out) if out else None
    rec("T4-2 colour backdrop #112233 in the top / bottom bars", a is not None and abs(a[100, 540] - [17, 34, 51]).max() < 8 and abs(a[1800, 540] - [17, 34, 51]).max() < 8,
        "" if a is None else f"pixel={a[100, 540].round().tolist()}")
    # (g) --still on both canvases (fit + blur) + normaliser
    for cname, src in (("youtube", port), ("reels", land)):
        out, log = run(f"t42_still_{cname}", job_for(src, cname, "fit", {"type": "blur", "strength": 5}), still=True)
        a = frame(out) if out else None
        W, H = CV[cname]
        side = a[:, :150] if cname == "youtube" else a[:150]
        rec(f"T4-2 --still blur on {cname}", a is not None and a.shape[:2] == (H, W) and side.mean() > 25, "" if a is not None else log[-200:])
    n = t4_cfg({"backdrop": {"type": "weird", "strength": 99, "path": 5}})["backdrop"]; m = t4_cfg({"backdrop": {"type": "BLUR", "strength": "x"}})["backdrop"]
    rec("T4-2 normaliser: backdrop type / strength / path", n["type"] == "color" and n["strength"] == 10 and n["path"] == "" and m["type"] == "blur" and m["strength"] == 5, f"{n} {m}")
finally:
    shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
# ---- T4-3 timing (pure unit checks, no render) ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import builtins, contextlib, io                                # noqa: E402
from mt.templates.t4.timing import t4_ask_when, t4_stagger, t4_time, t4_warn_reset  # noqa: E402
def quiet(fn, *a):
    buf = io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buf): r = fn(*a)
    return r, [l for l in buf.getvalue().splitlines() if "[!]" in l]
CUES = [(1.0, 2.0, "hello everyone"), (3.0, 4.0, "open the project now")]
t4_warn_reset()
r, w = quiet(lambda: (t4_time({"start": 2, "end": 5}, [], 10, 4), t4_time({"start": "0:03", "end": "0:06"}, None, 10, 4)))
rec("T4-3 explicit start / end (numbers and mm:ss strings)", r == ((2.0, 5.0), (3.0, 6.0)) and not w, f"{r} {w}")
r, w = quiet(lambda: (t4_time({"trigger": "open the project", "hold": 2}, CUES, 10, 4), t4_time({"trigger": "open the project"}, [CUES, []], 10, 4)))
rec("T4-3 trigger found (one cue list, or a list of streams); hold / default_hold", r == ((3.0, 5.0), (3.0, 7.0)) and not w, f"{r} {w}")
t4_warn_reset()
r, w = quiet(lambda: [t4_time({"trigger": "never said"}, CUES, 10, 4) for _ in range(3)])
rec("T4-3 trigger missing -> None, warned once", r == [None] * 3 and len(w) == 1, f"{r} {w}")
r, w = quiet(lambda: t4_time({"trigger": "never said", "start": 2, "hold": 1}, CUES, 10, 4))
rec("T4-3 trigger missing but start given -> start is used", r == (2.0, 3.0), f"{r} {w}")
r, w = quiet(lambda: (t4_time({"start": 1, "hold": 2.5}, None, 10, 4), t4_time({"start": 1}, None, 10, 4), t4_time({"start": 1, "hold": 0}, None, 10, 3), t4_time({"start": 1, "end": 9, "hold": 2}, None, 10, 4)))
rec("T4-3 hold / default_hold / end wins over hold", r == ((1.0, 3.5), (1.0, 5.0), (1.0, 4.0), (1.0, 9.0)), f"{r}")
r, w = quiet(lambda: (t4_time({"start": 8, "end": 20}, None, 10, 4), t4_time({"start": 9, "hold": 5}, None, 10, 4), t4_time({"start": 10}, None, 10, 4), t4_time({"start": 12}, None, 10, 4), t4_time({"start": 2}, None, 10, None)))
rec("T4-3 end beyond duration clamped; start at / after the video end -> None; no hold -> to the end", r == ((8.0, 10.0), (9.0, 10.0), None, None, (2.0, 10.0)), f"{r}")
junk = [None, 5, "x", [], {}, {"start": "abc"}, {"start": float("nan")}, {"start": True}, {"trigger": 7}, {"start": 1, "end": "zz", "hold": "q"}]
r, w = quiet(lambda: [t4_time(j, CUES, "bad", "bad") for j in junk])
rec("T4-3 junk never raises", r == [None] * len(junk), f"{r}")
rec("T4-3 t4_stagger", t4_stagger(3, 0.2) == [0.0, 0.2, 0.4] and t4_stagger(0, 1) == [] and t4_stagger("x", 1) == [] and t4_stagger(2, "q") == [0.0, 0.0] and t4_stagger(2, -1) == [0.0, 0.0])
def scripted(inputs, **kw):
    it = iter(inputs); old = builtins.input; builtins.input = lambda *_: next(it)
    try: return quiet(t4_ask_when, "Name tag", 4.0)[0]
    finally: builtins.input = old
a, b = scripted(["1", "0:05", "3"]), scripted(["2", "open the project", ""])
rec("T4-3 t4_ask_when (time mode / phrase mode, default hold)", a == {"start": 5.0, "hold": 3.0} and b == {"trigger": "open the project", "hold": 4.0}, f"{a} {b}")

job = job_for(ctmp / "none.mp4", "reels", "fill", captions=[dict(CAPC, srt=str(ctmp / "ar.srt"))])
n_t4, n_all = len(quiet(t4_caption_items, job, 4.0)[0]), len([i for i in quiet(build_items, job, 4.0)[0] if i.sprite.info.get("pill_h")])

def scripted2(first, n=40):
    it = itertools.chain(first, itertools.repeat("")); old = builtins.input; builtins.input = lambda *_: next(it)

    finally: builtins.input = old

# ---- T4-5 captions 'glow' (unit checks; stills are checked by eye - see T4_NOTES) --------------------------------------------------

GL = {"t4_style": "glow", "ref": "canvas", "mode": "phrase"}

bad_g = []
for cv in ("reels", "youtube"):
    W, H = CV[cv]

            f = ctmp / f"g_{nm}_{pos}.srt"
            f.write_text("1\n00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,000\n" + txt + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
            t4_warn_reset()
            items, w = quiet(t4_caption_items, job_for(ctmp / "none.mp4", cv, "fill", captions=[dict(GL, srt=str(f))]), 4.0)

                bad_g.append(f"{cv}/{nm}/{pos}: {len(items)} items warn={w}"); continue
            it = items[0]; a = np.asarray(it.sprite.getchannel("A"))
            ys, xs = np.where(a > 60)
            x0, x1 = it.cx - it.sprite.width / 2.0 + xs.min(), it.cx - it.sprite.width / 2.0 + xs.max()
            y0, y1 = it.cy - it.sprite.height / 2.0 + ys.min(), it.cy - it.sprite.height / 2.0 + ys.max()
            sf = 48 * min(W, H) / 1080.0

                bad_g.append(f"{cv}/{nm}/{pos}: ink box x {x0:.0f}-{x1:.0f} y {y0:.0f}-{y1:.0f} outside safe")

# ---- T4-6 captions 'twoline' (unit checks; stills are checked by eye - see T4_NOTES) ------------------------------------------------

def _np_job(cv, frame_on, tag=None, lst=None, **extra):
    t4 = t4_cfg({"canvas_mode": cv, "frame": {"on": frame_on}, "nameplate": tag or {"on": False}, "corner_list": lst or {"on": False}})
    return dict({"canvas": list(CV[cv]), "t4": t4}, **extra)
def _ink(it, W, H):
    full = Image.new("L", (W, H), 0)
    full.paste(it.sprite.getchannel("A").point(lambda v: 255 if v > INK_THR else 0), (int(it.cx - it.sprite.width / 2), int(it.cy - it.sprite.height / 2)))
    return full
